6.10 Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board (I&VSB) of ICAI has been constituted in the year 2024-2025 to act as an instrument for the empowerment of the profession and aims to facilitate a fair corporate regime with the best global practices in the sphere of Insolvency and Valuation. Also, the Board has been formed to focus on advancement of activities for formulation, implementation and recognition of ICAI Valuation Standards 2018. The Insolvency & Valuation Standard Board (I&VSB) plays a crucial role in maintaining financial stability by establishing and promoting transparent and consistent standards in the fields of insolvency and valuation.

Significant Achievements and Initiatives

(I) Submission of suggestions to Insolvency and Bankruptcy Board of India (IBBI) on various Discussion Papers as brought out by IBBI/MCAThe Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board has conducted Roundtable Discussions jointly with The Indian Institute of Insolvency Professionals of ICAI (IIIPI) of ICAI to discuss the proposed amendments. The Suggestions on the following Discussion Papers were prepared, compiled and submitted to IBBI: • Discussion Paper on Monitoring Committee under CIRP • Discussion paper on Reducing Compliance by Review of CIRP Forms submitted by Insolvency Professionals

(IPs) to IBBI • Discussion Paper on Mediation by the operational creditors (OCs) before approaching Adjudicating Authority

(AA) for filing Section 9 application • Discussion Paper on Review of Grievance Redressal and Enforcement Framework and Rationalisation of Timelines Regarding Authorisation for Assignment • Discussion Paper on amendments to Insolvency and Bankruptcy Board of India (Liquidation Process) Regulations, 2016 and Insolvency and Bankruptcy Board of India (Voluntary Liquidation Process) Regulations, 2017 • Discussion Paper on Issues Related to Real Estate • Discussion Paper on Format for progress report during Liquidation Process • Discussion paper on amendments to Insolvency and Bankruptcy Board of India (Insolvency Resolution Process for Corporate Process) Regulations, 2016 • Discussion Paper on MSME Registration and Disclosure Framework under CIRP • Discussion paper on "Strengthening the process of issuance of record of default by Information Utility

(II) Publications released by Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board, ICAI • White Paper on International Convention on Insolvency Resolution and Valuation – RESOLVE-2024The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board (I&VSB) of ICAI, in collaboration with IBBI, hosted the International Convention on Insolvency Resolution and Valuation—RESOLVE 2024—on July 12-13, 2024, in New Delhi. The event gathered global experts to discuss challenges and promote alignment of Indian insolvency practices with international standards. Featuring nine technical sessions and seven panel discussions, the convention focused on enhancing the insolvency framework. A White Paper summarizing key insights, issues, and recommendations was prepared to guide improvements in the Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016. • Issues and Recommendations arising out of the discussions at International Convention on Insolvency Resolution and Valuation - RESOLVE 2024- On the basis of the detailed discussions at the Convention, the Board published a document specifically focusing on the Issues and Recommendations arising from the deliberations during RESOLVE-2024. This document will help the Regulators, Policy Makers, Judiciary and other stakeholders in taking further actions and in plugging the gaps and providing solutions. Further, it will provide valuable insights to all the stakeholders including Insolvency Professionals and Registered Valuers and serve as important resource for Insolvency Professionals and Valuers, guiding them towards improved practices in insolvency resolution and valuation. • Judicial Pronouncements under Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016 Series-5- This publication is a part of an ongoing series aimed at enhancing awareness and understanding of the evolving judicial interpretations of the IBC framework. This highlights summary of landmark rulings by the Supreme Court, High Court, NCLAT, and NCLT, which have clarified provisions, resolved ambiguities, and strengthened the legal framework for insolvency resolution. Through this series, the I & VSB equips stakeholders with practical insights into procedural intricacies, fostering greater expertise and efficiency in insolvency resolution practices. • A Study on the Efficacy of IBC, 2016 in meeting deadlines and maximizing value during the Corporate Insolvency Resolution Process (CIRP)- This research study provides an analysis of delays in the Corporate Insolvency Resolution Process (CIRP), their impact on asset realization values, and the prevalence of defunct companies under CIRP. By examining these factors and offering actionable recommendations, the study aims to provide as a guide towards enhancing the efficiency and efficacy of insolvency proceedings, fostering a more robust resolution framework in India. • Educational Material on ICAI Valuation Standard 302- Intangible Assets- This Educational Material aims to provide clarity and guidance on the complex process of valuing intangible assets, which often form a significant part of a company's value. This publication offers practical insights, case studies, and detailed guidance on valuation practices in accordance with ICAI Valuation Standards 2018. It supports consistent and uniform practices across India and contributes to enhancing the efficiency and expertise of valuers, ultimately fostering transparency and stability in financial markets. • Summary of Amendments in Regulations under The Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016 (Revised February 2025 Edition)- This publication aims to provide a concise overview of key regulatory updates to the IBC framework introduced in 2023 and 2024. These amendments, addressing various aspects such as insolvency professionals, information utilities, corporate insolvency, liquidation, and inspection processes, are designed to enhance the Code's efficiency and implementation. Notable changes include allowing the committee of creditors to approve audits during resolution processes, mandating separate bank accounts for real estate projects, revising fees for creditor representatives, and ensuring timely committee meetings. • Unicorns, Soonicorns, and the Myth of Valuation - A Critical Examination of Startup Growth and Sustainability - Series 1- This booklet delves into the dynamic world of startups, exploring their valuation paradigms from ideation to unicorn status. With India emerging as a global hub for innovation, this booklet examines the factors influencing startup valuations, including the challenges posed by untested products, uncertain growth projections, and intangible assets. The booklet features real world examples from prominent companies like Swiggy, Flipkart, and Ola, illustrating their distinct valuation journeys and strategic decisions that have defined their paths to success. • Frequently Asked Questions on The Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016 (Revised February 2025 Edition)- This publication is brought by I & VSB of ICAI in collaboration with Insolvency and Bankruptcy Board of India. The publication has been designed in a question-and-answer format which comprehensively covers questions to assist our members and other stakeholders to have conceptual clarity on the various aspects of Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016. Further appropriate case laws have been included so as to help in understanding the provisions under the Code clearly and also know about the practical aspects. • Handbook on Do's and Don'ts for IPs under The Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016 (Revised February 2025 Edition)- This handbook guides Insolvency Professionals (IPs) on their roles, responsibilities, and regulatory compliance under the IBC and IBBI Code of Conduct. It helps IPs understand and fulfill their duties with due diligence, reflecting recent amendments and judicial rulings on best practices and restrictions to ensure effective and compliant insolvency resolution. • Handbook on Claims under The Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016 (Revised February 2025 Edition- This revised publication offers detailed guidance on the submission, admission, verification, and management of claims under the Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code (IBC), covering processes like CIRP and liquidation. It includes provisions, timelines, forms, FAQs, and case laws to help insolvency professionals handle claims efficiently and compliantly. The update reflects recent amendments and judicial rulings on claim inclusions and acceptance deadlines. • Handbook on Resolution Plan under The Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016 (Revised February 2025 Edition)- This revised Handbook guides professionals on the complex aspects of Resolution Plans under the Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code (IBC). It details the submission and approval processes, mandatory contents, and the invitation of expressions of interest from resolution applicants. The updated edition incorporates recent amendments and judicial rulings, providing Insolvency Professionals with essential insights for efficient insolvency resolution in India. • Question Bank on IBBI Limited Insolvency Examination (Revised February 2025 Edition)-The Question Bank aims to assist professionals in preparing for the Limited Insolvency Examination conducted by the IBBI.

This examination is a key requirement for becoming an Insolvency Professional, a vital role in the insolvency and bankruptcy resolution process under the Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016. This revised edition incorporates amendments and updates since its last edition, ensuring alignment with the latest regulatory changes. This publication serves as a practical and handy resource to support aspiring insolvency professionals in understanding the Code and excelling in their professional journey. Due to various amendments and changes, this publication has been revised. • Background Material for the Certificate Course on The Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016- The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board (I&VSB) of ICAI has released Background Material for the Certificate Course on the Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code (IBC), 2016. This resource helps participants understand insolvency laws, resolution processes, and regulations, aiding them in exam preparation. It covers key topics like resolution planning, creditor meetings, and bid evaluation, ensuring Insolvency Professionals maintain transparency and legal compliance. The initiative aims to develop skilled professionals and strengthen trust in the financial system.

(III) Development of ICAI Valuation Standards for other two assets class i.e., "Plant & Machinery" and

"Land & Building" in addition to "Securities or Financial Assets" The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board is developing Valuation Standards for Land & Building, Plant & Machinery, and Securities & Financial Assets. Drafts have been prepared for Plant, Machinery and Equipment, Real Property Interest, and Development Property, with the formulation process currently underway.

(IV) Second Edition of International Convention on Insolvency Resolution and Valuation "RESOLVE2024" held on 12th - 13th July 2024 at Bharat Mandapam, New Delhi The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board of ICAI organized the second edition of RESOLVE-2024: An International Convention on Insolvency Resolution and Valuation. The event, held on 12th & 13th July at Bharat Mandapam, ITPO, New Delhi, embraced the theme "Enabling Resolution, Maximizing Value." It proved to be a compelling platform for the exchange of global best practices in the fields of insolvency and valuation. The convention was attended by approximately 500 participants, making it a significant milestone in our journey towards promoting excellence and innovation in these fields. The convention featured nine technical sessions and seven panel discussions, covering a wide range of topics focused on maximizing value and enhancing the insolvency framework.

(V) Quarterly e-Newsletter "Valuation Edge"The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board has been bringing quarterly e-Newsletter namely "Valuation Edge" for keeping the members consistently informed and well-versed in the evolving landscape of Valuation and Insolvency laws. The Board has released 5 editions of e-Newsletter for the quarter ending March 2024, June 2024, September 2024, January 2025 and March 2025.

(VI) Monthly Knowledge Corner by Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board has been bringing Knowledge Corner on IBC in the form of News Updates and Case Law Updates on a monthly basis covering important case analysis based on the decisions by Supreme Court, High Courts, NCLAT and NCLT on issues under The Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016.

This initiative aims to keep professionals updated with the latest developments and legal interpretations related to the IBC, enhancing their understanding and expertise in this domain. The Board has released 14 editions of Knowledge Corner for the period from 1st April 2024 to 31st May 2025.

(VII) Monthly Quiz on IBC and Valuation The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board has introduced a monthly quiz as a part of its strategic initiatives to enhance knowledge of members and to promote active engagement. As an incentive for active involvement, the top three participants are being rewarded with publication of the Board. This initiative does not only promote learning but also encourages members to stay updated and engaged with current developments in the field of insolvency and valuation. The Board has released 14 series of Monthly Quiz for the period from 1st April 2024 to 31st May 2025.

(VIII) Announcements by I&VSB The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board issues announcements on I&VSB Page of ICAI website to update stakeholders on the latest amendments, circulars, and notifications released by the IBBI. 40 Announcements have been released during the period.

(IX) Online Certificate Course on The Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016 in association with IBBI The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board conducts Certificate Course on the Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016 which aims to educate participants on the Code's provisions, associated Rules and Regulations to gain clear understanding of the Insolvency & Bankruptcy Code, 2016, and its practical applications. The Board has organized 11 batches of Online Certificate Course on Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016 for the period from 1st April 2024 to 31st May 2025 and has trained more than 950 members during this period.

(X) ICAI-SAFA Webinars organised by Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board, ICAI jointly with Indian Institute of Insolvency Professionals of ICAI (IIIPI) ▪ 1st ICAI-SAFA webinar on the topic "International Insolvency-Best Practices" held on 20th March 2024 with participation of more than 860 members including 15 members from 15 different countries, reflecting a truly global engagement. ▪ 2nd ICAI-SAFA Webinar on the topic "Cross Border Insolvency Framework under IBC" held on 24th June 2024 with more than 650 participants.

(XI) Two-day Residential Refresher Courses organised by Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board, ICAI The Insolvency & Valuation Standards Board organized four two-day Residential Refresher Courses in 2024 at Srinagar, Bhubaneswar, Dehradun, and Andaman & Nicobar Islands. These courses focused on promoting ICAI Valuation Standards 2018, Valuation, and Insolvency & Bankruptcy laws. Participants gained valuable insights and explored opportunities for Chartered Accountants in the fields of valuation and insolvency.

(XII) Training Programmes/ Workshops/ Conferences/ Webinars/ Seminars on Insolvency and Bankruptcy laws, Valuation and ICAI Valuation Standards 2018 The Board had organised the varios National Conference/ International Conference/ Seminar with a view to promote and to create awareness about ICAI Valuation Standards 2018, Valuation and Insolvency and Bankruptcy Laws.

6.11 Committee on International Taxation The Committee on International Taxation (CITAX) was previously merged with the Direct Taxes Committee in the year 2023-24. However, with the introduction of the Income-tax Bill, 2025, the scope of work in both Direct Taxes and International Taxation would expand significantly. Additionally, with the diploma course on international taxation and certification course on UAE Corporate tax running concurrently in either physical or virtual mode, manifold publications, and a number of seminars, conferences etc. related to direct taxes and international taxation, there is a need for focus on both domains. In view of this, International Taxation Committee has been demerged from Direct Taxes Committee for the year 2025-26. The Committee on International Taxation plays a vital role in addressing matters related to international tax laws and policies. It examines tax laws, rules, regulations, circulars, and notifications issued by the Government pertaining to international taxation and submits suitable suggestions through detailed memoranda. It represents on OECD/UN papers, promotes research, organizes seminars and study tours, publishes technical material, and coordinates with overseas bodies, aiming to enhance member knowledge in international taxation.

Details of Programmes undertaken by the Committee to promote Courses As part of its ongoing efforts to enhance the professional knowledge and competencies of ICAI members, the Committee on International Taxation has organized Diploma in International Taxation (DIIT) and the Certificate Course on UAE Corporate Tax.

The details of the courses conducted are as follows:

Course Batch Mode From-To 3rd Batch Physical (Pune) 12-04-2025 to 17-05-2025 Certificate Course on UAE Corporate Tax 34th Batch Physical (Delhi) 24-05-2025 to 10-08-2025 Diploma in International Taxation (DIIT) These programs have received an enthusiastic response from Chartered Accountants across the country, reflecting a strong and growing interest in acquiring specialized knowledge in international taxation and emerging global tax frameworks.

6.12 Corporate Laws and Corporate Governance Committee (CL&CGC) The Corporate Laws & Corporate Governance Committee (CL&CGC) is to empower the profession and promote a fair corporate regime through global best practices. The Committee actively strengthens the regulatory framework by engaging with the Ministry of Corporate Affairs and providing regular representations and suggestions to enhance stakeholders experience and on matters pertaining to the Companies Act 2013. With a focus on knowledge enhancement, the Committee strives to empower members of the Institute to stay current on corporate laws, enabling them to navigate the evolving landscape of corporate governance effectively.

Significant Achievements and Initiatives

(I) Suggestions on SEBI Consultation Paper The Securities and Exchange Board of India (SEBI) released a Consultation Paper on 7th February 2025, concerning secretarial compliance reports, appointment of auditors, and related party transactions (RPTs) of listed entities. The Committee reviewed the consultation paper, which proposes amendments to the SEBI

(LODR) Regulations, aimed at enhancing transparency based on Advisory Committee on Listing Obligations and Disclosures (ACLOD) recommendations. Accordingly, the Committee submitted its suggestions to SEBI.

(II) Suggestions on the Income-tax Bill, 2025 In response to a request from the MCA, the ICAI submitted its comments on provisions under the Companies Act, 2013 relevant to the Income-tax Bill, 2025. Detailed suggestions finalized after extensive deliberations and reviewed by an expert group were forwarded for onward submission to the Hon'ble Select Committee of the Lok Sabha.

(III) Supporting MCA in Smooth Transition of new E-Forms in MCA21 V3 Portal In collaboration with the Ministry of Corporate Affairs (MCA), the CL&CGC of ICAI is playing a key role in the rollout of 38 new e-Forms, including important Annual Filing Forms, under the MCA21 Version 3 portal, set to go live on July 14, 2025. To ensure a smooth implementation and raise stakeholder awareness, a nationwide series of Outreach Programs was held from June 4 to 14, 2025. ICAI has also nominated Chartered Accountants and officials for User Acceptance Testing (UAT) and to assist in addressing technical issues on the portal. As a strategic partner in regulatory capacity building, ICAI is jointly conducting seminars and webinars with MCA across various locations to facilitate knowledge sharing and resolve compliance issues.

(IV) Recommendation on Simplification of Companies and LLP Rules Under the pre-legislative consultation policy, the Ministry of Corporate Affairs sought ICAI's inputs for the comprehensive review of the Companies Rules, 2014 and LLP Rules. After a detailed analysis, ICAI submitted its recommendations aimed at simplifying compliance and enhancing regulatory efficiency to the Ministry of Corporate Affairs.

(V) Suggestions on Draft IEPF Authority Rules (Refund Process) The Committee submitted its observations on the Draft Rules concerning the refund process under the Investor Education and Protection Fund Authority (Accounting, Audit, Transfer, and Refund) Rules, 2016, to the Investor Education and Protection Fund Authority. These inputs focused on improving procedural clarity and ease of compliance.

(VI) Streamlining the PM Internship Scheme (PMIS) Implementation The PM Internship Scheme (PMIS), announced in the 2024-25 Budget, aims to provide internships to 1 crore youth over five years, in partnership with leading companies across sectors. To facilitate implementation, the MCA shortlisted 500 companies based on their CSR spend over the past three years. Responding to the MCA's request, CL&CGC of ICAI facilitated communication by identifying and collecting Single Point of Contact (SPOC) details from these companies. The Committee proactively coordinated with stakeholders and the MCA, to ensure companies could log in and submit internship opportunities on the PMIS portal.

(VII) Certificate Course on CSR Reporting and Impact Assessment The CL&CGC has organized a total of Twelve batches of the Certificate Course on CSR Reporting and Impact Assessment till May 2025. The course has a duration of 4 hours each day for 7 days and is conducted in online mode. The objective of the Course is to equip participants with the knowledge and skills to navigate the evolving CSR landscape. We are glad that more than 800 members have undergone the certificate course.

(VIII) Publications released by the Committee • Frequently Asked Questions on Securities and Exchange Board of India (Listing Obligations and Disclosure Requirements) Regulations, 2015 (Revised January 2025 Edition) • Frequently Asked Questions (FAQs) on Limited Liability Partnership Act, 2008 (Revised January 2025 Edition)

(IX) Independent Directors' Conclave organised by CL&CGC of ICAI To promote good governance and strengthen the role of Independent Directors, the Committee organized a series of Independent Directors' Conclaves in New Delhi, Ahmedabad, and Mumbai. These events served as significant platforms for dialogue, knowledge-sharing, and professional networking among Independent Directors and governance experts. These conclaves collectively contributed to capacity building, reinforced the importance of independent oversight in companies, and enhanced awareness on contemporary issues in corporate governance.

(X) Programmes/Conferences/Webcast/Courses organised by the Committee To enable the members of the Institute to update themselves and to keep pace with the changes, the CL&CGC organised one National Conference, 17 Webinars and 15 Seminars on Corporate Laws.

6.13 Committee on Commercial Laws, Economic Advisory & NPO Cooperative (CCLEA&NPO) The Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (ICAI) is committed to shaping the future of the accountancy profession by promoting excellence and acting as a trusted partner within the financial ecosystem. The Committee on Commercial Laws, Economic Advisory & NPO Cooperative (CCLEANC) of ICAI plays a vital role in equipping Chartered Accountants with in-depth knowledge and practical expertise across commercial laws, economic advisory, and the governance of Non-Profit Organizations (NPOs) and cooperatives. The Committee contributes to nation-building by empowering members to serve as reliable advisors in areas such as corporate governance, regulatory compliance, and dispute resolution. To strengthen professional capabilities, the Committee develops high-quality publications, conducts focused research, and utilizes digital platforms to disseminate knowledge. It regularly provides members with monthly updates, handbooks, and FAQs tailored to key laws and regulatory developments. Moreover, the Committee engages proactively with regulators and policymakers, representing the profession's interests and advocating for reforms that promote transparency and good governance.

Through these initiatives, CCLEANC enables Chartered Accountants to confidently address emerging challenges, drive economic growth, and leverage new opportunities in a dynamic global landscape.

Initiatives towards Partner in Nation Building undertaken by the Committee with Centre/State Govt./any Dept.

(I) Enabling opportunities in ADR sector while also collaborating with Global ADR Institutions The Committee on Commercial Laws, Economic Advisory & NPO Cooperative (CCLEANC) of ICAI is actively contributing to nation-building by promoting Alternative Dispute Resolution (ADR). It conducts specialized Certificate Courses on Arbitration and Mediation and has published guidance like "Professional Opportunities in Mediation for Chartered Accountants." To enhance global collaboration, ICAI initiated partnerships with CEDR and engaged with officials from the Singapore International Arbitration Centre (SIAC) and Singapore International Mediation Centre (SIMC). These efforts aim to strengthen international opportunities for Indian CAs in arbitration and mediation. A Memorandum of Understanding (MoU) with SIAC and SIMC is currently in progress.

(II) Setting up ICAI International ADR Centre The Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (ICAI) has initiated the establishment of the ICAI ADR International Centre to promote efficient commercial dispute resolution through arbitration, mediation, and conciliation. Aimed at fostering amicable settlements, the Centre will offer global-standard facilities and expertise, serving as a hub for professionals and businesses. This initiative supports ease of doing business and strengthens India's position as a global economic leader.

(III) New Certificate Course on Mediation The Council has approved a new Certificate Course on Mediation, designed in response to the growing need for alternative dispute resolution skills among Chartered Accountants and the enactment of the Mediation Act, 2023, aims to equip participants with essential mediation skills. These include conflict resolution techniques, negotiation strategies, and relevant legal knowledge, ultimately certifying them as mediators. Following approval of the Council, the Committee is now working on finalizing the course contents and other related matters, as suggested by the Council to implement the same.

(IV) Updation of Course Content of existing Certificate Courses:

The Committee is actively updating course content for its certificate programs in areas such as Anti-Money Laundering, FEMA, Real Estate, Cooperatives, NPOs, IPR Laws, and ADR. This ensures Chartered Accountants stay current with evolving laws and global best practices. The initiative reflects ICAI's commitment to enhancing professional competence and supporting nation-building through strong economic and legal frameworks.

(V) Exploring Collaboration with National law Universities The Committee is actively collaborating with National Law Universities to advance legal education as a significant initiative for nation-building. The Committee emphasizes integrating theoretical knowledge with practical expertise in areas like Commercial laws. The Committee organized the webinar "Decoding the Labour Codes: The Perspective of Organised and Unorganised Sectors" on 22nd August 2024, showcasing its commitment to addressing contemporary issues, addressed by Professor of NLU, Punjab.

(VI) Representation to Authorities • The Committee submitted key recommendations to enhance the digitalization and efficiency of Charity Commissioner and Joint Charity Commissioners' offices in Maharashtra. Suggestions include enabling online validation of audit reports via OTP/Digital Signatures, introducing online Change Reports with virtual hearings for complex cases, and fully digitizing trust budget submissions under the Maharashtra Public Trusts Act. The Committee also proposed streamlining login processes, enhancing the online portal's functionality, and resolving technical issues in form submissions and account filings, aligning with modern governance practices to improve transparency and ease of operations. • The Committee has submitted representation to the Central Registrar of Co-operative Societies (CRCS), Ministry of Cooperation, recommending the adoption of Reserve Bank of India (RBI) norms for empanelment of auditors for Multi-State Co-operative Societies. This aligns with ICAI's commitment to ensuring equitable and effective audits while addressing practical challenges faced by Multi-State Societies and Banks. The Committee suggested ICAI be entrusted with preparing the auditor panel, leveraging its expertise in empanelment for RBI and NABARD, thereby promoting consistency, fairness, and nationbuilding through improved governance in the cooperative sector. • The Committee has submitted feedback on SEBI's Consultation Paper on the Review of SEBI (Merchant Bankers) Regulations, 1992. The feedback emphasized the inclusion of Chartered Accountants as eligible Compliance Officers, highlighting their robust expertise in corporate and securities laws alongside financial and accounting proficiency. This proactive representation aims to safeguard and enhance career opportunities for Chartered Accountants, reinforcing their role in the regulatory landscape. • The Committee has submitted a representation to the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) requesting an extension of the deadline for the appointment of statutory auditors by Multi-State Cooperative Banks for FY 2024-25.

The request aims to provide banks adequate time to comply with revised regulatory requirements and appoint qualified auditors from the updated panel being finalized by the Central Registrar of Co-operative Societies. This step underscores ICAI's efforts toward fostering a robust and transparent financial system, contributing to nation-building.

(VII) Webinars under the "Know Your Laws" series The Committee has significantly contributed to the education of members of ICAI through its "Know Your Laws" series, organizing over 100 webinars to date. These sessions focus on educating Chartered Accountants about evolving commercial laws, enhancing their legal literacy, and equipping them to navigate complex legal frameworks. By empowering professionals with critical legal knowledge, the Committee fosters informed decisionmaking, strengthens governance, and supports India's economic and regulatory development.

(VIII) Physical Events on latest laws/Amendments The Committee has organized 8 impactful physical events as part of its nation-building initiatives on Prevention of Money Laundering Act, FEMA Practice, Role of Co-operative in Vision 2047: INDIA etc.

(IX) Publications • CAs' Handbook on Obligations and Privileges of Non-Residents under FEMA: Comprehensive guidance for non-resident compliance under FEMA. • CAs' Handbook on Drafting Power of Attorney: Detailed insights into drafting and legal provisions of Powers of Attorney. • FAQs on The Banning of Unregulated Deposit Schemes Act, 2019 (Revised 2024): Updated insights into safeguarding public interest from fraudulent schemes. • CAs' Handbook on Drafting of Wills: Simplified guidance for succession planning and drafting wills. • CAs' Handbook on Inbound and Outbound Investment under FEMA: Insights into cross-border investment regulations. • CAs' Handbook on Essential Drafting: Practical guidance for drafting critical legal and business documents. • Revised Background Material on Anti Money Laundering: Comprehensive material designed to enhance understanding and application of AML regulations. • Background Material on FEMA: Covers essential aspects of the Foreign Exchange Management Act to strengthen compliance and advisory roles. • Online Dispute Resolution- An International Perspective: Highlights global trends and practices in online dispute resolution with focus on cross-border implications. • An Introduction to the POSH Act, 2013: Overview of key provisions and compliance requirements under the POSH Act. • The POSH Act, & Opportunities:

CAs' Handbook for New Horizons Highlights professional roles for CAs in POSH compliance and advisory. • CAs' Handbook on Writs: Explains the types and uses of writs relevant to legal and compliance matters. • CAs' Handbook on Nomination Procedure and Guidelines: Guides CAs on legal procedures and best practices for making nominations.

6.14 GST and Indirect Taxes Committee (GST & IDTC) The Direct Taxes Committee (DTC) of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (ICAI) is a key committee dedicated to matters related to direct taxation. It actively engages with the Government, the Central Board of Direct Taxes (CBDT), and other relevant forums by making representations on legislative amendments and pressing issues concerning direct taxes. A primary focus of the Committee is to enhance the knowledge and skills of ICAI members in direct taxation through the publication of new materials, updates to existing resources, and the organization of seminars, webinars, and conferences.

(I) Initiatives towards Partner in Nation Building • Reinforcing GST policy making, implementation and research – Technical inputs ➢ Suggestions on Central Excise Bill, 2024 ➢ Pre-Budget meeting and submission of Pre-Budget Memorandum, 2024 ➢ Suggestions on the newly launched Invoice Management System (IMS) on GST Portal ➢ Suggestions on Harmonization of GST Rate Schedule on Services and the Classification of Services adopted for GST ➢ Suggestions on GST Annual Return [Form GSTR 9] and GST Reconciliation Statement [Form GSTR 9C] • Memorandum of Understanding with State Governments and NACIN The Institute, through GST & Indirect Taxes Committee, entered into Memorandum of Understanding (MoU) with Commercial Taxes Department, Andhra Pradesh on 8th April, 2024, Department of State Taxes, Assam on 4th February, 2025 and the Taxation Department, Meghalaya on 4th February, 2025 for capacity building and extending technical co-operation in research, assistance in policy making, consulting etc. Similar MoUs for capacity building were entered into with National Academy of Customs & Indirect taxes (NACIN) Zonal Campuses at Kolkata, Bhubaneswar, Patna and Shillong on 16th August, 2024 and NACIN ZC, Chennai on 30th August, 2024. The MoUs will facilitate imparting training to GST officers on a regular basis. • Supporting the capacity building initiatives of the Government - Capacity Building Programmes for Officials of States, Centre and Other Government Departments The Institute has also been assisting the Government in its capacity building initiatives by organizing customised training programmes on GST for the Officers of the Central Tax, State Tax/Union Territory Tax and other Government departments as also by providing expert faculty for the trainings organised by the Government. During the reporting period, the Committee facilitated/organised forty-six (46) Capacity Building Programmes on GST for the GST Department of State Governments, Central Government, NACIN, and Other Government Departments upskilling more than 2100 Officers. • Outreach Programme for Trade and Industry The Committee organised an Outreach Program on the GST Amnesty Scheme on 27 th March, 2025 at ICAI Thiruvananthapuram, based on the request from the Chief Commissioner, CGST, Thiruvananthapuram Zone. The session was attended by 108 participants from trade and industry. • Meetings with functionaries of the Central and State Governments Various meetings were held during the reporting period with various Commissioners of State Tax/GST and the other senior officers of Central and State Governments to apprise the Officials about the various initiatives taken by the ICAI with regard to GST and discuss ways and means through which the GST & Indirect Taxes Committee can offer proactive support to the Government in the area of GST.

(II) Building Brand ICAI - Important Events • National GST Symposium 2024 - Driving GST Success: Insight, Innovation and Integration for a good and simple tax The Committee has been organising the National GST Symposium annually from the last two years. The National GST Symposium 2024 was organised at Kolkata on 9th & 10th January, 2025. Shri Shrawan Kumar, IRS, Chief Commissioner, CGST & CX, Kolkata was the Chief Guest and Shri Devi Prasad Karanam, IAS, Commissioner, Directorate of Commercial Taxes, West Bengal was Guest of Honour. Thirty-eight senior CGST & SGST/UTGST Officers like Principal Chief Commissioners/ Chief Commissioners/ Principal Commissioners/ Commissioners/ Additional Commissioners/ from 19 States all over India participated in the Symposium. A Senior Vice President of GSTN also participated in the Symposium. • Conclave on Indirect Taxes Litigation Before Tribunal The Committee organized a Conclave on Indirect Taxes Litigation before Tribunal in New Delhi on 21st December, 2024 to enhance Chartered Accountants' skills in drafting, representation, and procedural compliance before the Tribunal. Hon'ble Justice Shri C Hari Shankar, Delhi High Court was the Chief Guest and Hon'ble Justice Shri Dilip Gupta, President, CESTAT was the Guest of Honour. The Conclave was a pioneering event with over 20 esteemed Members of CESTAT from different benches across the country present on one platform and sharing their insights on judicial perspectives, best practices and practical approaches to effective advocacy in indirect taxes litigation. • National Conferences on GST During the reporting period, the Committee organised ten (10) National Conferences on GST/Indirect Taxes at Indore, Ernakulam, Pune, Delhi (2), Chennai, Mumbai, Rajkot, Kolkata and Navi Mumbai which were attended by over 5,300 members.

(III) GST Knowledge Sharing - Initiatives • Enhancement in the Committee's website In line with the Committee's objective to enhance GST knowledge sharing, a new feature was added on the website of the Committee namely, "GST Statutes" 27th August, 2024. This feature provides free access to all GST Acts, Rules, Notifications including the amended notifications, Circulars, Orders, Instructions/Guidelines etc. Further, the minutes of the GST Council meetings, GST Press Releases, GSTN Advisories are also available under GST Statutes for ready references of the stakeholders. This step further reinforces the Committee's commitment to facilitating accessible and comprehensive resources on GST. • Certificate Course on GST With an objective of building the capacities of the members in GST in a systematic manner and enhancing their skills, the Committee launched a Certificate Course on GST on 28th April, 2017. Since then, the Committee has been regularly organising the Course across India. During the reporting period, thirteen (13) batches of the Course were organised physically which were attended by 569 members. Further, four batches of the Course were organized virtually which were attended by 493 members. The Assessment Tests for the Certificate Course on GST was conducted online on 13th October, 2024 and 9th February, 2025. A total of 515 members successfully passed the aforesaid Assessment Tests. • Live Webcasts/Webinars During the reporting period, the Committee organised 18 live webcasts/webinars on various contemporary topics in GST which were attended by more than 21,000 participants. • CPE events on GST With a view to update the members with the latest developments in GST & other indirect taxes, the Committee organised 76 CPE events (workshops, seminars etc.) in the reporting period upskilling more than 7500 members. • ICAI Newsletter on GST With an objective of providing updated information of GST to all stakeholder(s), ICAI launched its dedicated Newsletter on GST in April 2017. During the reporting period, thirteen Editions of the ICAI –Newsletter on GST were published. • GST Updates GST law is an extremely dynamic law with multitude of notifications/ circulars/ orders/ instructions being issued frequently. In order to keep the members abreast with such frequent changes, GST Updates - containing the summary of such changes - are prepared by the Committee as and when any notification/circular/order/instruction etc. is being issued by the CBIC and mailed to over 50,000 users registered on the website of the Committee as also uploaded on the website of the Committee. During the reporting period, 42 GST Updates were prepared and circulated. • E-learning on GST The Committee re-launched its E-learning on GST on 23rd May, 2025 at Digital Learning Hub covering various important aspects of GST. This facility is available to all members free of cost facilitating them in learning the basics of GST anytime & from anywhere. During the short period of less than a month, it has been subscribed by 23,322 members.

(IV) Publications The Committee developed a new publication during the year namely, "Departmental GST Audit of Entities with Multiple GSTINs". Further, following publications were revised during the reporting period: • Background Material on GST • GST Act (s) and Rule(s)- Bare Law (Three Revisions) • Guide to CA Certificates • Technical Guide on GST Annual Return (Form GSTR 9) • Handbook on Reverse Charge under GST • Handbook on Exempted Supplies under GST

6.15 Ethical Standards Board (ESB) The Institute of Chartered Accountants of India has constituted the Ethical Standards Board as the designated standard-setting authority for ethical governance within the profession. The Board is vested with the mandate to develop and implement ethical standards and other authoritative pronouncements applicable to Chartered Accountants. In fulfilling its mandate, the Board endeavours to evolve a dynamic and contemporary Code of Ethics and ethical framework for members, while upholding the time-honoured principles of excellence, independence, and integrity, thereby safeguarding the dignity and interests of the members. The objective of the Ethical Standards Board is to formulate and promulgate ethical standards for Chartered Accountants that align with internationally recognized best practices, subject to compliance with applicable domestic laws. Through this alignment, the Board aims to enhance the quality, consistency, and reliability of services rendered by Chartered Accountants, thereby reinforcing public confidence and trust in the profession.

Since its inception, the Board has diligently engaged in establishing and maintaining standards that regulate the professional conduct and qualification of ICAI members. In addition to its core responsibilities, the Board actively undertakes initiatives aimed at augmenting members' professional competitiveness and dynamism. Moreover, the Board administers a grievance redressal mechanism concerning instances of unjustified removal of members as auditors. This function serves as a protective measure to uphold the independence of members, ensuring that those adversely affected by unwarranted auditor removals have access to equitable recourse and justice.

(I) Significant Achievements • Global Ethics Day was celebrated on 16th October, 2024 at New Delhi, while programmes were conducted for awareness at all Branches/Regional Councils in the month of October on this occasion. Special edition of CA Journal for October Month dedicated to theme of Ethics was issued. Ethics quiz and string of other measures for dissemination of ethics among members were organized. • CA Connect portal, the listing platform of members and CA Firms at the platform of the Institute, has been redesigned and new features have been incorporated to the portal. As part of popularization of CA Connect Portal, a programme was organized on 1st September 2024 at Ranchi. A new Logo and tagline of CA Connect Portal was released on this event. • The Ethical Standards Board has developed a booklet on "CA Ethics Plus- Knowledge sharing on ethics for Chartered Accountants". The booklet contains compilation of provisions and pronouncements on contemporary ethical issues and serves as a ready reckoner. • The Ethical Standards Board has issued a brochure on CA Connect Portal "Listing of Chartered Accountants. By ICAI. For all CA Services". This brochure contains the features of CA Connect Portal, its benefits and advantages for members/firms and for stakeholders. • Revised Edition of "FAQs on Ethical Issues" was brought out in February 2025 • The Ethical Standards Board has issued clarifications/announcements and FAQs on the queries raised frequently by the members and questions posed on various issues. • The Ethical Standards Board contributed monthly awareness column "Know Your Ethics" in the CA Journal, covering key topics such as fee issues, directorship, KYC norms, documentation, and ethical aspects of professional conduct to enhance awareness and compliance among members. • More than 650 queries of members on various professional ethical issues were addressed and resolved in the given period. Besides, replies to enquires made by the Regulators and Government departments were given.

(II) Seminars/Programmes organized by the Board on Code of Ethics • The Ethical Standards Board organized 18 Webcasts for bringing awareness of provisions on Code of Ethics and ethical issues among the members. • Various seminars and programmes on ethics were also conducted during the year.

(III) Meetings of Committee on Professional Ethics and Independence of South Asian Federation of Accountants (SAFA) Three Meeting of SAFA (South Asian Federation of Accountants) Committee on Professional Ethics and Independence was held on March 1, 2024 at Pokhara, January 10, 2025 at Karachi, and January 30, 2025 at Delhi reinforcing ICAI's regional engagement on ethical standards.

(IV) IESBA Meetings attended by ICAI delegates IESBA (International Ethics Standards Board for Accountants) – NSS (National Standard Setters) Meeting held on May 2 & 3, 2024 at New York, IFAC Offices which was attended by Chairman, Ethical Standards Board in person.

6.16 Digital Accounting and Assurance Board (DAAB) The Digital Accounting and Assurance Board (DAAB) traces its roots to the year 2000, when ICAI established the Committee on Information Technology (CIT) to spearhead the profession's IT initiatives. In its first decade, CIT focused on raising IT awareness, introducing the Information Systems Audit (ISA) course, and building basic technology infrastructure for members. In 2009, the committee broadened the scope by including forensic accounting, XBRL implementation, e-learning, and greater technology integration into audit practices. Recognizing the accelerating digital transformation in finance, ICAI reconstituted this committee as the Digital Accounting and Assurance Board (DAAB) in 2019, with an expanded mandate covering digital competencies, forensic standards, blockchain, AI, and global partnerships. This evolution over two decades laid the groundwork for a proactive body attuned to the emerging challenges and opportunities of the digital era.

(I) Key Milestones and Initiatives • DISA Launches and Upgrades: The journey began with the launch of the Post-Qualification course on Information Systems Audit (ISA) in 2000, which provided Chartered Accountants specialized training in IT auditing. This program later, undergone multiple syllabus revisions (DISA 1.0, 2.0, 3.0) to keep pace with technological advances. Over the years, DISA gained formal recognition from regulators notably the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) and market authorities enabling DISA-qualified members to conduct bank system audits as experts. Today, DAAB is in the process of rolling out DISA 4.0, a comprehensive upgrade that will incorporate cybersecurity frameworks, data privacy (DPDP Act) compliance, regulatory technology (RegTech), and other emerging areas into the curriculum. • Forensic Accounting and Fraud Detection (FAFD) Course: Recognizing the growing need for forensic expertise, ICAI launched the Certificate Course on Forensic Accounting and Fraud Detection (FAFD) in 2009, equipping members with investigative skills supported by hands-on training through forensic labs, including one established in Noida in 2017. As the field evolved, DAAB continually upgraded the course— most notably aligning it in 2023 with ICAI's world-first Forensic Accounting and Investigation Standards

(FAIS), now mandatory for forensic assignments. In 2024, DAAB initiated the course's transition into a fullfledged Diploma in Forensic Investigation (DFI), which received initial approval from the Ministry of Corporate Affairs and is set for public exposure before final clearance—marking a major step in advancing formal forensic education. • Forensic Accounting and Investigation Standards (FAIS): A landmark achievement of the DAAB era has been the development of Forensic Accounting and Investigation Standards (FAIS) – a comprehensive framework to guide practitioners in forensic accounting engagements. Released in series (totalling 20 individual standards and 3 overarching documents) and refined through expert input, the FAIS were officially issued with mandatory implementation from 2023 onwards. These standards cover the entire gamut of forensic accounting, from evidence collection and analysis to reporting, and serve as a definitive benchmark for quality and consistency in forensic services undertaken by chartered accountants. • Digital Competency Maturity Model (DCMM): To help the profession assess and improve its adoption of technology, the Board developed the Digital Competency Maturity Model (DCMM) Version 1 and in 2019, DCMM 2.0, its implementation guide and a tool was released which provides a structured model with defined levels and an implementation guide for firms to evaluate their digital capabilities. Further, it was made part of Peer review also. By benchmarking areas such as IT infrastructure, software tools, process automation, and digital skills, DCMM has enabled audit firms (especially small and medium practices) to identify gaps and roadmaps for digital transformation. The model's relevance has extended beyond India – it has been showcased in international forums and adapted by global accounting groups as a tool for enhancing digital practices in the accounting profession. • International Outreach : From its early years, DAAB has extended its impact internationally. In the mid2000s, ICAI's IT Committee began delivering ISA courses in Sri Lanka and Nepal, supported by MoUs with ICASL and ICAN. These collaborations later expanded to include forensic accounting training. By 2025, ICASL expressed interest in adopting ICAI's forensic standards, reflecting their global relevance.

DAAB has also conducted virtual DISA classes for professionals in both countries and hosted international participants in its programs. A key example was the customized FAFD training for Bank of Bhutan auditors in October 2024. These efforts have strengthened ICAI's global standing in digital accounting and assurance. • Government and Regulatory Collaborations : Over its 25-year journey, DAAB has played a pivotal role in integrating digital audit and forensic practices into governance through close collaboration with government bodies and regulators. Key milestones include early engagement with the CAG to introduce IS audit in public audits and RBI's recognition of the DISA qualification for statutory bank IS audits. More recently, DAAB has supported law enforcement by training agencies like the Gujarat Police Economic Offences Wing and recommending ICAI's forensic standards. In 2024, the Board signed MoUs with Rashtriya Raksha University and NFSU to collaborate on cybersecurity, AI, and forensic research. DAAB has also conducted specialized training for agencies such as SFIO, CBI, CAG, ICLS Academy, DGGI, and major PSUs like SBI, BOB, HPCL, and IOCL—reinforcing its role as a trusted advisor in advancing financial transparency and digital governance. • Publications ➢ Technical Guide on Digital Assurance ➢ Concept paper on Embracing Robotic Process Automation Opportunities and Challenges for Accountancy Profession ➢ Concept Paper on Blockchain Technology Adoption Trends and Implications for Accountancy Profession ➢ Concept Paper on "ABCD of Technology" 2025 ➢ Digital Competency Maturity Model for Professional Accounting Firms - Version 2.0 and Implementation Guide ➢ Report on Automation in Finance Functions- lessons from India and the UK ➢ Early Signals of Fraud in Banking Sector (Revised 2018 Edition) ➢ A Guide to Internet of Things - Basics & Applications ➢ Guide to Cloud Computing for Accountants 2025 ➢ Compendium of Forensic Accounting and Investigation Standards (As on 1st July, 2023) ➢ Implementation Guide on Forensic Accounting and Investigation Standards (As on 1st July 2023)

(II) Strategic Initiatives In its 25th year, DAAB continued to drive innovation and capacity-building through several high-impact initiatives, aligning with its long-term strategic goals: • Upgrading Qualifications In 2024–25, DAAB prioritized upgrading ICAI's post-qualification courses to equip members with cutting-edge skills. The DISA program is being upgraded to Version 4.0 with enhanced content on cybersecurity, DPDP Act compliance, RegTech, and IT governance. Exam frequency has been increased to allow faster course completion. The FAFD course is being elevated to a Diploma in Forensic Investigation (DFI), with a proposal submitted to the Ministry of Corporate Affairs. Additionally, a new "DISA+" refresher course is being developed to keep DISA alumni updated on emerging trends in IT audit and technology. • Conferences and Thought Leadership:

In 2024–25, DAAB organized several flagship conferences to promote thought leadership in digital accounting and assurance. Key events included Future Proof Forensics in Mumbai, a two-day summit on financial fraud investigation with 135+ participants from banks, regulators, and enforcement agencies; Pragyotsav-2024 in Jaipur, a national conference on internal audit and technology attended by 2,000 professionals and inaugurated by the Hon'ble Vice President of India; and Gyan se Vigyan in Belagavi, focused on digital transformation in accountancy with 476 participants. Looking ahead, DAAB has announced the Digital Transformation (Dx) Finance Summit 2025 in Hyderabad, themed "Fortifying Finance: Forensics, Cybersecurity & Digital Trust," targeting top finance and tech leaders to explore cutting-edge developments in AI, blockchain, cyber governance, and ICAI's forensic standards. • Capacity Building and Training In 2024, DAAB expanded its training outreach through impactful national and international programs. It conducted a specialized Financial Crime Investigation training for 64 officers of the Gujarat Police's Economic Offences Wing, recommending adoption of ICAI's FAIS standards. In June, a first-of-its-kind Residential Digital Forensics Refresher Course at NFSU provided 44 members with hands-on lab training. Internationally, DAAB organized a 5-day customized FAFD program for senior auditors from the Bank of Bhutan.

Additionally, four specialized training sessions were delivered for Indian Corporate Law Service (ICLS) officers, covering cybersecurity, forensic accounting, and digital forensics—strengthening ICAI's role in advancing governance and capacity-building across sectors. • Digital Learning and Faculty Development In 2024, DAAB enhanced its technological offerings through e-learning and updated resources. It launched a free Advanced Blockchain Technology module on the ICAI Digital Learning Hub, providing members with practical insights into blockchain and crypto-assets. To ensure course relevance, a joint Faculty Meet for ISA and FAFD was held in April 2024, reviewing curricula and preparing to upgrade FAFD into a diploma. A Faculty Identification Programme also added 13 new trainee faculty to strengthen the training ecosystem.

Additionally, DAAB updated key publications, including the Guide to Cloud Computing and the ABCD of Technology paper, reinforcing its commitment to keeping members current with emerging tech trends. • Strengthened Partnerships Collaboration with institutions continued to be a hallmark of DAAB's strategy this year. ICAI signed Memorandums of Understanding with Rashtriya Raksha University (RRU) and National Forensic Sciences University (NFSU) – both prestigious national institutions under the Ministry of Home Affairs – to jointly promote education and research in emerging areas such as artificial intelligence, cyber security, forensic accounting, and digital governance. These partnerships envisage collaborative teaching, development of new courses, and knowledge exchange that will contribute to a technology-driven ecosystem and support India's goals for a digitally empowered economy. The Board's international engagement also remained strong: its courses and events drew participants from abroad, and virtual batches of DISA continued for overseas members in regions like South Asia. Such initiatives underscore the global reach and appeal of DAAB's programs, reinforcing ICAI's reputation as a leader in digital accounting and assurance training.

(III) Membership Growth Over 25 years, DAAB's programs have created a wide base of digitally skilled professionals within ICAI. By March 31, 2025, a total of 35,982 members have qualified the Information Systems Audit course (ISA/DISA).

Likewise, 14,206 members have been certified in Forensic Accounting and Fraud Detection (FAFD) as of March

2025. This substantial growth in specialized qualifications reflects the success of DAAB's initiatives and the appetite among chartered accountants for upskilling in high-demand areas. The ever-increasing number of DISA and FAFD alumni demonstrates how the Board's efforts have strengthened the profession's collective capability to perform IT audits, cybersecurity assessments, forensic investigations, and other technology-driven services.

(IV) Collaborations and Outreach The Digital Accounting and Assurance Board (DAAB) of ICAI, celebrating its 25-year journey from an IT Committee in 2000 to a leading board in 2025, has played a key role in advancing digital excellence in the profession. Through collaborations with global bodies like ISACA, ICASL, and Hyperledger, DAAB has enriched education in information systems audit, forensics, and blockchain. Its standards are being adopted internationally, and its partnership in launching EFAA's global competency model highlights its leadership. DAAB's initiatives have empowered thousands of CAs and strengthened public trust in the profession's digital readiness. Looking ahead, ICAI aims to build on this legacy through continued innovation and global collaboration.

7. PROFESSION DEVELOPMENT COMMITTEES

7.1 AI in ICAI The "AI in ICAI" Committee is a New Committee which has been formed for the First Time in Council Year 202425, and it aims to integrate AI into ICAI operations, enhancing services for members, students, and employees. It continues for the Year 2025-26. It promotes AI awareness through digital materials, webinars, and workshops, and engages with AI experts and stakeholders. The committee will identify AI tools, collaborate with startups, and develop an AI research lab. Additionally, it seeks international AI partnerships, explores professional opportunities for members in AI, and incorporates AI tools into educational content for enhanced financial reporting and compliance solutions. The Committee has launched an ICAI CA GPT for Members and Students which provides significant benefits to both members and students by offering quick, AI-driven solutions to complex queries related to accounting, auditing, tax, and compliance. It enhances learning by acting as a study aid for students, offering instant access to key concepts, standards, and regulations. For members, it simplifies research, assists with technical queries, and streamlines decision-making processes. Additionally, it supports real-time problem-solving and helps in staying updated with the latest industry trends, improving both educational and professional outcomes.

The Committee has launched a Certificate Course on AI AICA Level 1 which offers several key benefits. • Foundational AI Knowledge: Provides a strong understanding of AI concepts relevant to accounting and finance. • Practical AI Applications: Teaches real-world AI use cases. • Career Advancement: Equips members with in-demand AI skills, enhancing professional opportunities. • Hands-on Learning: Offers exposure to AI tools and platforms through interactive sessions and case studies. • Certification: Recognizes participants as AI-proficient, boosting credibility in the profession.

Achievements • Digital Publication on ChatGPT, Copilot have been published. More such publications are in the pipeline. • Committee on AI in ICAI is organizing Webinars, Seminar and Workshops on AI. A total of 80-90 events have been conducted thus far. • Live Interaction of AI Leaders with RC and Branch Management Committee Members, Overseas Chapters has been conducted by the Committee. • ICAI CA GPT ➢ Phase 1 – 1st July 2024 - launched on with a repository of 16 GPTs created for the first phase, covering 16 Committees/Departments of ICAI. The relevant documents for these committees have been uploaded onto the CA GPT, allowing members to access the specific GPTs for these committees/departments. o Accounting Standards o Auditing and Accounting Standards o Corporate Laws and Corporate Governance o Direct Taxes o Expert Advisory o GST and Indirect Taxes o Members and Student Services o Sustainability Reporting Standards o Acts and Regulations o Board of Internal Audit and Management Accounting o Digital Accounting and Assurance Board o Ethical Standards o Financial Reporting and Review Board o Insolvency and Valuation Standards Board o Peer Review Board o ICAI ejournal ➢ Phase 2 - 10th September 2024 - The Constitution of India GPT was launched. ➢ Phase 3 = 28th September 2024 - Launch of two more GPTs by the name of UAE VAT and UAE Corporate Tax Assistant ➢ Phase 4, AI in ICAI launched Industry Forum GPT wherein Annual Reports of more than 5000 listed companies are uploaded for the benefit of members. This is a first of its kind initiative anywhere globally. ➢ Phase 5 – 2nd February 2025 - AI in ICAI Launched GPT on The Digital Personal Data Protection Act (DPDP Act) ➢ Phase 6 – 1st April – GPT on Bank Audits was launched ➢ Phase 7 – GPT on Guide for Non-Corporate, LLP, NLP Reports launched on 28th May 2025 • Launch of ICAI CA Student GPT ➢ ICAI CA GPT for Students was launched on July 16, 2024, in Hyderabad, coinciding with ICAI AI Innovation Summit. ➢ three tailored versions specifically designed for students at the Foundation, Intermediate, and Final levels. These GPTs include previous question papers, study materials, and the syllabus as published on the ICAI website. • 4 Hours Workshop for ICAI Employees-700+ Employees participated in Workshop conducted on 21st March 2024. • Expression of Interest for Registration of AI Faculty has been floated, and vendors will be onboarded as per process • Committee has organized ICAI AI Innovation Summit 2024 on 16th and 17th July 2024 in Hyderabad. More than 2000 delegates attended the same and 35 Speakers were enlisted for various sessions for the Conference. • The Next edition will be held on 18th and 19th July 2025 in Pune. • Two Day National Conference on AI held at Bhilai on 17th and 18th August 2024. 250 Participants attended the same • AI Forum for Accounting and Finance held in Chennai on 23rd August 2024. 700 Participants attended the same • AI Forum for Finance and Accounting held in Bengaluru on 22nd August 2024. 400 Participants attended the same. • Committee Website ai.icai.org is launched and up and running • Twitter Account and WhatsApp channel is Active for Committee • Trademark Registration for AI in ICAI is Accepted and advertised. • AI Faculty Meet was held on 29th April,2024 • The first Faculty Identification Programme (FIP) was conducted on 21st, 22nd, 23rd, and 24th August 2024. • The second FIP was held on 9th and 10th October 2024. A total of 80+ participants registered, with 60+ attending the session physically. Approximately 25 participants were shortlisted and invited for an online demonstration via Zoom. • The third FIP was held on 4th and 5th January 2025. Over 180 participants applied, out of which 120+ were shortlisted for attending the FDP. • Fourth FIP was held on 16th and 17th May 2025. Over 70 participants attended the same. • A total of 40 faculty members have joined the AI CA Certificate Course. • The May 2024 edition of the ICAI e-journal, which focuses on AI, features 3-4 articles contributed by AI faculty and hackathon participants has been released Certificate Course on AI – AICA Level 1 • The Council approved Certificate Course on AI. • The AICA Certificate Course was launched on 16th July,2024 in AIS Innovation Summit at Hyderabad. • First Certificate Course (Batch 1, Batch 2, Batch 3 & Batch 4) was held on 21st, 22nd and 23 Aug,2024) at ICAI COE Hyderabad. • Total 420+ AICA Certificate Course Schedule till Date. • Total 15000+ Participants Received AICA_Level1 Certificate • Total 20000+ Participants Registered AICA -Level1 Registered • AICA Course Scheduled in 26 States and 100 Cities. • Level 2 of the Course has been approved and will be launched shortly • Conduction of ICAI AI Innovate Challenge for ICAI Employees om 24th May 2024 AI Hackathons Season 1 • AI in ICAI Committee commenced the AI Hackathon Season 1 on April 5, 2024. • A total of 60 participants competed across six hackathons. • From these, 18 participants advanced to Semi-Final 1 and Semi-Final 2. • seven finalists competed in the Grand Finale on July 16, 2024, in Hyderabad. • The results of the Grand Finale were declared on August 2, 2024, during the launch of Hackathon Season 2.

Season 2 • The Season 2.0 of AI Hackathon has commenced in the months of November 2024. • 12 Panellists competed in the Grand Finale held on 7th March 2025 in which 3 winners have been declared Season 3 • The Season 3.0 of AI Hackathon has commenced on 21st March 2025. • The eight preliminary rounds have been held. • The two semi finals are lined up for 6th June 2025 and 13th June 2025 • The Grand Finale will be held during the ICAI AI Innovation Summit 2025.

AI in ICAI has commenced Series of Book Writing on AI • The following three books will be launched shortly: ➢ Ethics in AI ➢ Privacy in AI ➢ Impact of AI on Indian Economy

7.2 Committee for Members in Industry and Business (CMI&B)

(I) Campus placement Programmes for newly qualified Chartered Accountants Campus Placement Programme (held twice a year) provides a platform to both the Newly Qualified Chartered Accountants (NQCAs) and the organizations looking to hire the best available talent to fulfil their Human Resource requirement. This programme, being a one stop solution, offers a unique opportunity to the employers to interact with the NQCAs, peruse the particulars of a huge pool of promising professionals and recruit the suitable one(s) who is found to be better than the best. • 59th edition of Campus Placement Programme – February-March 2024 59th edition of Campus Placement Programme – February March 2024 The 59th edition of Campus placement Programme covered the candidates who have passed final examination held in Nov 2023 examinations and Candidates who passed final examination at the immediate previous examination, but did not register for the then campus programme, were allowed to register. The interviews were successfully completed at 9 major (Ahmedabad, Bengaluru, Chennai, Hyderabad, Jaipur, Kolkata, Mumbai, Delhi, and Pune) and 17 smaller centres (Bhubaneswar, Bhopal, Chandīgarh, Coimbatore, Durgapur, Ernakulam, Indore, Kanpur, Lucknow, Nagpur, Noida, Patna, Ranchi, Raipur, Thane, Vadodara, and Visakhapatnam). 12.49 LPA • Placement Programme for Women CAs ICAI has been taking several measures to develop capacities of women members to effectively utilize their professional knowledge and expertise. In the spirit of empowering women members, ICAI organized Second Placement programme for Woman CAs jointly with Woman Member Empowerment Committee (WMEC).

(II) Career Ascent Programme for placement of experienced Chartered Accountants CMI&B offers a customised Campus Placement Programme for the experienced Chartered Accountants which provides a platform to the Chartered Accountants to secure career advancement in leading organizations and an excellent opportunity for organizations to recruit best of finance and accounts professionals. This drive is an extended dimension to the existing campus placement programme, being regularly undertaken by CMI&B for the Newly Qualified Chartered Accountants, twice a year. The Indian companies participating have to offer a minimum CTC as per the following experience criteria:
