(III) Annual ICAI Awards to recognise CA achievers in industry and business The ICAI established the ICAI Awards in 2007 to acknowledge members who have demonstrated excellence and displayed an enduring commitment to achieving heights in their roles as CA professionals. These awards aim to honor individuals by exhibiting excellent skills, dedication, enthusiasm, leadership, and the ability to deliver the best practices that others strive to emulate.The Award process for this year commenced on September 14, 2024, with the registration of nominations under various categories, including CA Business Leader, CA CFO & CA CXO, CA Entrepreneur, CA Global Achiever, and CA Special Recognition (for specially abled). A total of 118 nominations were received.The Nomination Committee, convened on January 10, 2024, in Mumbai.

(IV) 40 UNDER 40 – CA BUSINESS LEADERS The Committee for Members in Industry & Business (CMI&B), in association with CNBC-TV18 and Business Standard, successfully organized the prestigious CA Business Leader–40 Under 40 Season 3 awards to recognize outstanding young Chartered Accountants under 40. Held on January 17, 2024, at the Jio World Convention Center, Mumbai, the event honored exemplary CA business leaders for their excellence and contributions to the profession. Judged by an eminent panel of industry experts, the ceremony also featured insightful discussions on key economic issues, making it a memorable celebration of talent and leadership within the CA community.

(V) Facilitating CPE Study Circles for Members in Industry The CMI&B facilitates the organizations having more than 25 Chartered Accountants working therein to form a forum/ study Circle to take forward the continuing professional learning for them. These Study Circles help them to organise in house training sessions and training programmes on topics of contemporary relevance for them.

(VI) Programmes and Events organized by CMI&B during the Year Notable Initiatives • Management Development Programs for CA rank holders • HR Meets • Industry-specific webinar • Other programs and seminars • Report on Research Study: Performance of Industries in Various States

(VII) Management Development Programme for CA final rank holders CMI&B organised 9th Batch of Management Development Programme for the CA Final rank holders of Nov 2024 examinations to improve their managerial & leadership skills to enable them to attain good pay packages. This batch of MDP was organized from 18th to 27th January 2024 in association with Indian School of Business.

(VIII) Industry Specific Webinars Organized by CMI&B during the Year 2024-25 The Committee for Members in Industry and Business organized industry-specific webinars to disseminate the knowledge about the evolving regulations, technological advancements, industry best practices & knowledge, problem solving skills, continuing education and career advancement amongst the members of ICAI.

(IX) ICAI launches new platform to empower members in the industry ICAI a premier statutory body with over 400,000 members and 985,000 students globally, plays a key role in advancing the CA profession. The Committee for Members in Industry & Business (CMI&B) strengthens ties between ICAI and CAs in industry. Under its leadership, the CMIB Portal was launched, featuring a Placement Portal for campus placements and the upcoming CA Job Portal—a comprehensive recruitment platform offering resume building, job recommendations, interview tools, and more. These initiatives aim to empower members with future-ready resources for career growth in a rapidly evolving professional landscape.

(X) Masterclass on Leadership in association with Indian School of Business (ISB) The Committee, in collaboration with the Indian School of Business (ISB), conducted a Masterclass on Leadership for ICAI members, offering pre-recorded lectures and online self-assessments via the ISB portal. Upon completing the required learning hours, participants received a joint certification from ICAI and ISB. The program, covering critical thinking, storytelling, and self-leadership, was offered at a nominal fee of ₹1,000 + GST and free for campus placement registrants (Aug–Sep 2024 and Feb–Mar 2025). Corporate group registrations were also encouraged. Concluding with Batch 8 in January 2025, the program saw over 27,000 registrations across free and paid categories.

(XI) Management Development Program in association with ISB, Hyderabad The Committee has conducted the MDP for the 11th batch & 12th batch in association with ISB (01st batch) for the first time in this year for rank holders in the month of July 2024 & November 2024 with Centre of Excellence Hyderabad. Earlier, the Committee had conducted the similar program with various IIMs in the past year's rankholders. This program is specifically designed for all the rankholders of ICAI to enhance their managerial and leadership abilities, which in turn will improve their overall understanding of business in addition to their strong technical proficiency.

(XII) CFO NEXT Forum 2024 The Committee for members in industry and business organized CFO Next Forum programs at myriad locations of India. This program aims to empower CFOs to be more effective leaders and strategic partners within their organizations by providing them with the knowledge, connections, and insights they need to thrive in today's complex business environment. The event brings together the CFOs and Business Leaders to share their experiences and transformational change.

(XIII) Business Leaders Forum 2024 Business Leaders Forum 2024 was organized by Committee for members in industry and business on 26th November which was hosted by ICAI, Singapore Chapter at Pan Pacific, Raffles Boulevard, Singapore. The evening comprised of two panel discussions particularly relevant to the current economic climate. The first panel, focused on Economic Trends and Investment Opportunities in India, will delve into the promising avenues for businesses looking to tap into India's growth potential. The second panel, on Building Strategic Alliances and Maximizing Returns, will explore the critical role of partnerships in driving sustainable growth. The event provided an ideal platform for fostering meaningful connections and exploring synergies between Indian and Singaporean businesses.

7.3 COMMITTEE FOR AGGREGATION OF CA FIRMS The Committee for Aggregation of CA Firms (CACAF), constituted in 2024-2025 by the ICAI, stands at the forefront of reshaping the landscape for CA firms in India. Tasked with a pivotal role, CACAF's vision is driven by a commitment to conduct an exhaustive review and revision of various existing guidelines pertaining to CA firms, ensuring they are not only relevant but also adaptive to the continuously evolving professional and regulatory landscape thereby enhancing their operational efficacy and global competitiveness. CACAF seeks to empower CA firms to expand their expertise, scale operations and navigate the dynamic professional landscape effectively. The Committee's strategic initiatives are designed to streamline processes, reduce policy and procedural impediments and cultivate an ecosystem of resilience and excellence. Through stakeholder engagement, capacity building and continuous feedback, CACAF ensures that CA firms are well-equipped to thrive in an increasingly competitive and evolving global market.

(I) Significant Achievements • Revision of the ICAI (Merger & Demerger of CA Firms) Guidelines 2024:

The Committee has made concerted efforts in reviewing and revising the Merger and Demerger Guidelines.

Comprehensive efforts have been made, ranging from conducting surveys to the exposure draft of the revised guidelines, interactive brainstorming sessions with CA Firms to threadbare discussions in various meetings of the Committee. The ICAI (Merger & Demerger of CA Firms) Guidelines, 2024 were approved in July 2024 and have been notified in the Gazette of India. It has been endeavoured to streamline the guidelines both at policy and procedural levels. • Introduction of the ICAI (Aggregation of LLPs) Guidelines 2024:

The Committee has also brought a set of new guidelines for Aggregation of the Indian CA Firms. It basically provides an opportunity for an LLP, Indian CA Firm to be a partner with another LLP, a CA Firm. This particular concept among LLPs will serve as a vital tool for strategic growth. By these guidelines, firms can significantly expand their capabilities, broaden their market presence, and achieve substantial operational efficiencies. This strategic move allows them to leverage combined resources, diversify service offerings and enhance competitive advantages, ultimately driving long-term success and sustainability in an increasingly competitive landscape. The ICAI (Aggregation of LLPs) Guidelines, 2024 were approved in July 2024 and have been notified in the Gazette of India. • Draft Guidelines for Overseas Network:

In alignment with ICAI's vision of expanding India's footprint on the global stage, the Draft Guidelines for Overseas Network is under finalization. These guidelines aim to establish a structured and regulated pathway for networking and collaboration. This initiative is designed to foster global connectivity, enhance professional opportunities for Indian CA firms, uphold the integrity and quality of services delivered as well as to support the evolving needs of the profession in an interconnected world.

(II) Committee Events • Seminar on Growth Strategies for CAs in practice was held on 25th May, 2025 which included a session on the "Need for Networking/Merger of CA Firms" and a detailed discussion on "Merger/Demerger & Networking Guidelines," and "Success Story of Indian CA Firms. • Series of Brainstorming Sessions ': '-On June 3 and June 6, 2024, the Committee for Aggregation of CA Firms (CACAF) held Virtual Brainstorming Sessions titled : , to engage stakeholders, particularly CA firms, in reviewing the exposure drafts on proposed amendments to the Merger & Demerger Guidelines and the new LLP Firm Network Model. Released on May 22, 2024, these drafts were the central focus of discussions aimed at exploring practical implications and implementation strategies. The sessions included interactive online polls with MCQs on mergers, networking and proposed amendments, which provided immediate feedback and enriched the dialogue with real-world experiences, ensuring that the guidelines are refined to meet the evolving needs of the CA profession effectively. • Interactive Session on 'Aggregation of CA Firms' with Secretary MCA- On June 21, 2024, the Committee for Aggregation of CA Firms (CACAF) organized two interactive sessions on the theme 'Aggregation of CA Firms', featuring the Secretary and Joint Secretary of the Ministry of Corporate Affairs.

These sessions witnessed enthusiastic participation from CA firms of varying sizes and served as a significant platform for dialogue between the profession and policymakers. The government officials addressed participants' concerns and reaffirmed their commitment to fostering a supportive ecosystem for the growth, consolidation, and global competitiveness of Indian CA firms. • Consultative Outreach Meetings: Four Consultative Outreach Meetings were conducted through virtual mode on 31st July, 2024 with CA firms to have consultation on the draft proposed guidelines on Overseas Networking, wherein 136 practicing CAs participated and gave their comments during the meetings as well as subsequently through emails. • Outreach Programs on 'Network to Net Worth: Unleashing Growth Potential for CA Firms- The Committee for Aggregation of CA Firms (CACAF), in collaboration with NIRC and its branches, organized a series of Outreach Programs titled "Network to Net Worth: Unleashing Growth Potential for CA Firms" in Faridabad, Patiala and Shahdara, Delhi. The events featured key sessions by Chairman, CACAF, and Past President, ICAI, focusing on growth strategies through mergers, LLP aggregation, and strategic collaboration. These programs emphasized scaling CA firms via networking and concluded with interactive discussions and strong professional engagement.

7.4 Committee for Members in Practice (CMP) The Committee for Members in Practice (CMP) a non-standing Committee of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (ICAI) established with a primary objective to revitalize the practice portfolios of practitioners and firms through a suite of targeted initiatives. Aligned with the overarching mission of the ICAI to cultivate globally competent professionals, the Committee for Members in Practice (CMP) undertakes multifaceted initiatives aimed at capacity building of Chartered Accountants in practice. In pursuit of its mandate to strengthen the practice portfolio of members and enhance their visibility, the CMP regularly organizes refresher courses, workshops, conferences, seminars, certificate courses, brainstorming sessions, and interactive forums.

(I) Capacity Building Measures:

Tthe Committee identified five key emerging areas to open new professional avenues for practicing Chartered Accountants. The five key identified areas are as follows: • GST Litigation • Real Estate Super Consultants • MSME Consultants • Global Consultancy Services, • Virtual CFO As a foundational step, efforts have been focused on developing a robust resource pool comprising Master Trainers, Content Writers, and Trainers, who will be instrumental in designing and delivering structured capacity building programmes in emerging and critical areas of practice. Further the Committee in collaboration with GST & Indirect Taxes Committee has developed a specialized six days training program on GST Litigation, aimed at empowering professionals to competently represent clients and effectively manage complex GST-related legal matters. To take this initiative forward a 3 days training programme has been conceptualized with the objective of building this trainers' pool to have nationwide outreach.

(II) Activities/Initiatives: • "Practice ki " Practice ki ", launched by the Committee for Members in Practice (CMP) of ICAI, is a flagship professional development initiative aimed at enhancing the core competencies of Chartered Accountants in practice. Held at the Centres of Excellence in Jaipur and Hyderabad, the program focuses on key areas such as practice management, client communication, financial planning, and the use of advanced technologies like AI, cybersecurity, and data analytics. With 17 successful batches and strong participant response, the program has now been upgraded from a 3-day residential format to a more flexible 4-day residential/non-residential structure, ensuring greater accessibility and wider reach across the CA community. • Series of Webinars on Grow Your Practice The Committee for Members in Practice (CMP) of ICAI is organizing a series of webinars under the theme

"Grow Your Practice." These webinars are designed to equip Chartered Accountants with the latest tools, strategies, and insights to expand and enhance their professional practices. Covering a range of topics such as business development, digital transformation, client management, and emerging opportunities, the series aims to empower members to navigate the evolving business landscape effectively. Each session brings together industry experts and seasoned practitioners who share practical knowledge and innovative approaches to help CAs build a sustainable and thriving practice. Through this initiative, CMP underscores its commitment to fostering growth and excellence within the CA community. Till date the Committee for Members in Practice has successfully organised 36 such webinars. • Online Weekly Trainings on Audit and Accounting Tools for the Practicing members The Committee for Members in Practice (CMP) of ICAI has organized a weekly training focused on audit and accounting tools, for Chartered Accountants in practice. These sessions are aimed at enhancing the technical proficiency of CAs by providing hands-on training in the latest audit and accounting software's and methodologies. Each week, participants delve into various aspects of audit automation, data analytics, and documentation, accounting guided by experts who offer practical demonstrations and insights. The training is structured to help practitioners streamline their audit processes, improve accuracy, and maintain compliance with evolving regulatory standards. This initiative is equips the members with the necessary skills and resources to stay at the forefront of the profession in an increasingly digitalized audit and accounting environment. The Committee had successfully organized 29 such trainings on the tools such as Practice Management Software, Tally, Zoho, Any Audit- Audit Analytics Software, Assure AI- Audit Analytics Software. • Pathway to Practice - 7 days Residential Training Programme for COP Applicants The 7-Day Residential Training Programme is meticulously designed to provide a aspiring practitioner with the necessary tools, knowledge, and strategies to build a successful career in the ever-evolving domain of Chartered Accountancy. This program offers an immersive learning experience, combining expert-led sessions, Interactive workshops, and networking opportunities. The primary objectives of the training program is to explore innovative career pathways, stay updated with regulatory frameworks, or build a professional network, this training program serves as a transformative platform for future-ready practitioners. The inaugural batch of the programme was successfully conducted from 10th to 16th February 2025 at the Centre of Excellence (COE), Jaipur, ICAI. Currently, the Committee for Members in Practice (CMP) is in the process of revamping the structure of the programme to further enhance its effectiveness and relevance. • Promoting Networking & Other Consolidation Measures of CA Firms The Committee for Members and Practice is promoting Merger, Networking, Practice in Corporate Form for Practitioners/CA Firm spreading knowledge by conducting a flagship, Two Days National Networking Summit on Accelerating & Maximizing Practice. • National Conference, Seminars, Workshops organised by the Committee on Various Topics for the Capacity Building of the Members of ICAI The Committee organized approximately 68 capacity building programmes for practicing members, covering a wide range of topics including Income Tax, Indirect Taxation, Union Budget, GST, Bank Audit, MultiDisciplinary Partnership Guidelines, Harnessing Technology for Enhanced Auditing, Charitable Trusts, and Emerging Avenues for CAs in Practice. These programmes collectively benefited around 9159 members.

(III) Arrangements under "Member Benefit" of ICAI:

The Committee for Members in Practice extends various welfare measures to its members through arrangements with leading service providers for offering services at concessional rates, which the members may choose to avail from the committees website https://cmpbenefits.icai.org/. The Committee for Members in Practice has taken various initiatives to arrange for Beneficial Products and/or Services especially crafted for the members of ICAI.

7.5 Committee on MSME & Start-up The Committee on MSME and Startup is a non-standing committee of the ICAI, established to address the critical needs of the MSME and Startup sectors in India. Its primary objective is to enhance capacity through the development of sustainable frameworks tailored specifically for these sectors. By focusing on training, advocacy, and policy recommendations, the committee aims to bolster the growth and resilience of Indian MSMEs and startups, thereby fostering a conducive environment for entrepreneurship and economic development.

(I) Initiatives of the Committee on MSME & Startup, ICAI To enhance the capacity building measures of MSME & Startups, the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India

(ICAI) through the Committee on MSME & Start-up launched various initiatives on MSME & Start-up. • ICAI Startup Manthan- The Committee on MSME & Start-up organized ICAI Startup Manthan on 17th January 2025 at Jio World Centre, BKC, Mumbai. This program brings together thought leaders, investors, entrepreneurs, and key stakeholders in the startup ecosystem to discuss pertinent issues and share valuable insights. The event covered Government ecosystem, new avenues, funding challenges, exit strategies along with felicitation from Asia & India book of records for ICAI MSME & Startup yatra 2024. • Startup Sphere 2024- The Committee on MSME & Startup, along with related ICAI directorates, organized the three-day Startup Sphere 2024 event in Bengaluru from June 27-29, 2024, themed "Startup Synergy: Collaborate, Create, Conquer." The event showcased government support, funding sources, startup valuation, digital marketing, and celebrated International MSME Day. It brought together founders, investors, and leaders from 15 unicorn startups, including Swiggy and CRED, and featured prominent guests. Sessions covered fundraising, angel investing, venture capital, and Web 3.0 opportunities. The event also launched the ICAI MSME and Startup Yatra, a 100-city campaign promoting financial and tax literacy for startups and MSMEs. Supported by Startup India and other bodies, Startup Sphere 2024 reinforced ICAI's role in fostering innovation, entrepreneurship, and collaboration, contributing to India's economic and technological growth. • ICAI MSME & Startup Yatra 2024- The Committee, in collaboration with the Financial and Tax Literacy Directorate, launched the "ICAI MSME & Startup Yatra" on 27th June 2024, flagged off from Bengaluru on 29th June. This 100-day road journey covered around 19,000 km across multiple states until 19th October 2024, aiming to raise awareness about Central and State Government schemes and opportunities for MSMEs and Startups. The campaign, under the theme "Financial & Tax Literacy" and part of ICAI's "Hamara Sankalp Viksit Bharat" initiative, focused on building a sustainable Indian enterprise ecosystem by educating stakeholders on financial and tax matters, continuing the momentum from the MSME Yatra 2022. • Coffee Table Book on ICAI MSME & Startup Yatra 2024-Coffee Table Book on ICAI MSME & Startup Yatra 2024 was released during the celebration of the 75th Annual Day of ICAI, organized on the sidelines of the World Forum of Accountants (WOFA) on 2nd February 2025 at Yashobhumi, Dwarka. The Coffee Table Book provides comprehensive visual and narrative documentation of the ICAI MSME & Startup Yatra 2024, capturing key insights, initiatives, and milestones. • ICAI Pavilion at the Global Investors Summit (GIS) 2025, Bhopal- The Committee on MSME & Startup, ICAI, participated in the ICAI Pavilion at the Global Investors Summit (GIS) 2025 held on 24–25 February in Bhopal, Madhya Pradesh. With over 10,000 delegates in attendance, the summit offered a key platform to highlight ICAI's contributions to international trade, accounting, and capacity building for MSMEs and Startups. Positioned near the MP MSME Pavilion, the ICAI exhibit focused on its role in promoting entrepreneurship and featured key publications such as the background material for the Certificate Course on Startup and "A Bird's Eye View of MSMEs and SMPs in the Indian Economy," underscoring its commitment to the sector's development. • Certificate course on Startup- The Committee is conducting the Certificate Course on Startup is intended to equip Chartered Accountants to provide professional services as well as entering into Startup space themselves; to help achieve the national objectives. The Certificate course makes the Members of ICAI to be a business solution provider for Startup Sector. More than 1200 members participated in certification course on Startup. • Certificate course on MSME- The Committee conducted the Certificate Course on MSME intended to equip Chartered Accountants to provide professional services as well as entering into MSME space themselves; to achieve the national objectives. The Certificate course makes the Members of ICAI to be business solution providers for MSME Sector. Approx 1700 members appeared for Certificate Course on MSME. • Residential Refresher Course- The Committee has successfully conducted three batches of the Residential Refresher Course (RRC) at I-Hub, Ahmedabad on the theme "Startups & the Role of CAs as Investment Catalysts", receiving an encouraging response from approximately 200 ICAI members. This RRC provides a platform for networking, discussions, and exchange of insights with industry experts, investors, and regulatory bodies. • Zee Studio: Startup Special – 5-Episode Series- As part of its efforts to spotlight innovation and entrepreneurship, Zee Studio launched a five-episode series highlighting India's dynamic startup ecosystem.

The series featured expert insights and inspiring conversations, beginning with an overview of Startup Sphere 2024 and exploring emerging trends, challenges, and opportunities. It included exclusive interviews with Former Union Minister, discussing policy and innovation; Deputy MD of SIDBI, on financing and government support; CEO of MindMap Digital, on digital leadership; and entrepreneurs Co-founder & CEO of ProScaler.ai and Founder of SaffronStays, who shared their startup journeys. The series served as a valuable knowledge-sharing platform for aspiring entrepreneurs and stakeholders in the startup ecosystem. • National Startup Day - ICAI Startup Samvad Program- In alignment with the Committee's initiative to celebrate January 2025 as Startup Month, ICAI branches were organized Startup Samvad Programs to create awareness and build capacity for entrepreneurs, Chartered Accountants, and other stakeholders. Four branches Aurangabad, Ballari, Tirupur, and Ranchi successfully conduct the Startup Samvad Program during January 2025. • Start-up portal- The ICAI's dedicated portal https://startup.icai.org/ serves as a comprehensive platform connecting startups, Chartered Accountants (as business consultants), mentors, venture capitalists, and incubation centres. Key features include empanelment of mentors and investors to support startups, ICAI members offering consultancy in areas like corporate laws, valuation, and indirect tax, and showcasing CAled startup success stories to inspire others. The portal currently has over 200 startups, 78 investors, and 269 mentors registered, fostering collaboration and growth within the startup ecosystem. • ICAI MSME Portal- The Committee has a dedicated portal i.e https://msme.icai.org/ to providing an enabled ecosystem for networking, knowledge sharing, query resolution mechanism and exchange of services where any MSME can benefit from the expertise of the Chartered Accountants. There are 381 MSMEs registered on the portal. • Start-up Incubation Centres- Taking the journey forward to develop the startup ecosystem within ICAI, we have initiated the Startup Incubation Centres to facilitate start up ideas towards fostering the enterprising spirit and abilities of the Chartered Accountants. There are 10 Startup Incubation Centres at various cities established by ICAI.

(II) MOUs • MoU with IIM Lucknow Enterprise Incubation Centre, Noida, Gujarat Student Startup, and Innovation Hub (I-Hub) & BIL-RYERSON Technology Startup Incubator Foundation, AIC-Nalanda Institute of Technology Foundation, Bhubaneswar, Odisha, AIC Mahamana Foundation for Innovation and Entrepreneurship IM BHU, Varanasi for Capacity Building of Startups through ICAI Startup Gateway • MOU with the autonomous agency Facilitating MSMEs Government of Tamil Nadu for Capacity Building of MSMEs through ICAI MSME Ecosystem • MOU with the Government of Odisha through the intervention of the Micro, Small & Medium Enterprises Department for Capacity Building of MSMEs through ICAI MSME Ecosystem in the State of Odisha • MOUs with the State Governments for Capacity Building of MSMEs in the direction of Atmanirbhar Bharat • MOUs with the Universities, IIMs, IITs, State Governments, various departments of Central Government, State Government, organizations of repute, and other entities.

(III) Other Initiatives • MSME Booklet " A Bird's Eye View of Micro, Small & Medium Enterprises (MSMEs) and Small & Medium Practitioners in Indian Economy • Background Material for Certificate Course on Startup • Two Quarterly newsletters i.e ICAI Startup Manthan and MSME Updates

(IV) ICAI MSME Pathshala Series The MSME Pathshala Series is an ongoing initiative designed to equip professionals with comprehensive knowledge of the MSME and Startup sectors. It focuses on key areas such as policies, financial frameworks, and growth opportunities, while keeping participants informed on government initiatives and industry trends. Starting this Council Year, each weekly session highlights a specific State MSME Policy to help members understand region-specific schemes. Topics covered include CA opportunities as MSME Growth Partners and state policies of Karnataka, Haryana, Maharashtra, and Andhra Pradesh, fostering continuous learning and deeper engagement with the MSME ecosystem.

7.6 Committee on Promoting Work Life Balance The Committee on Promoting Work-Life Balance is a newly constituted in the Council Year 2025–26, body with the vision to empower ICAI stakeholders to harmonize their professional commitments along with personal wellbeing. The Committee is committed to nurturing a healthy work-life balance, encouraging sustainable career growth, and strengthening ICAI's standing as a globally recognized leader in the accountancy profession. Through this endeavor, the Committee aspires to cultivate not only enhanced professional excellence but also a more fulfilling quality of life.

(I) Details of Programmes undertaken by the Committee as part to promote Wellness To commemorate World Laughter Day on 4th May, 2025, the Committee hosted a lively and engaging celebration across ICAI's Regional Offices and Branches nationwide. Chartered Accountants from all corners of the country— from Jammu & Kashmir in the North to Trivandrum in the South, and from Dibrugarh in the East to Bhuj in the West—participated with enthusiasm and unity. Many Chartered Accountants showcased their talents through engaging performances such as mimicry and stand-up comedy in various branches. The event highlighted the uplifting power of laughter as a natural remedy for stress, fostering a sense of community and well-being among members.

(II) Webcast organized by the Committee to highlight the significance of physical and mental health as a key component of Promoting Work Life Balance In May 2025, the Committee organized a series of wellness-focused webinars for Chartered Accountants, covering diverse aspects of personal and professional well-being. Topics included redefining fitness "From Calculators to Kettlebells", life planning and balance, Work and Wellbeing:", mental resilience "Stronger Minds, Sharper Numbers" and relationship building beyond the workplace "Nurturing Connections" etc.

7.7 Continuing Professional Education Committee (CPEC) The Profession of Chartered Accountancy plays a critical role in enhancing transparency, ensuring compliance, and supporting effective decision-making across both public and private sectors. This profession represents foundation of financial integrity and strategic advisory within the global business environment. Chartered Accountants (CAs) are distinguished by their rigorous education, comprehensive professional training, and unwavering adherence to ethical and regulatory standards. Their expertise spans financial reporting, auditing, taxation, and business advisory services, making them integral to sound financial governance. ICAI through its Continuing Professional Education Committee, consistently strives to provide meaningful learning opportunities for its members. Continuing Professional Education (CPE) is not merely a mandatory requirement but a strategic investment in the professional growth of Chartered Accountants. It yields substantial returns in the form of enhanced technical proficiency, broader professional networks, and the confidence to address emerging complexities in the financial landscape. In a rapidly evolving business and regulatory environment—marked by the introduction of new accounting standards, dynamic compliance frameworks, and technological advancements—staying informed and up to date is essential.

The CPE Committee of ICAI equips members with the necessary knowledge and tools to adapt to these changes effectively. By doing so, it empowers Chartered Accountants to consistently deliver value to their clients and organizations, upholding the highest standards of professional competence and integrity. Furthermore, a strong emphasis on continuous professional development ensures that Chartered Accountants remain current with evolving regulations, technologies, and business practices.

(I) Significant Achievements and Initiatives • Organisation of National Level Programmes CPE Committee organised a 2-day National CA Conference 2024 on the theme ": Fostering Growth, Fuelling Success" hosted by Bhubaneswar Branch of EIRC of ICAI. The Conference was inaugurated by the Chief Guest, then Governor of RBI & Guest of Honour, CMD GAIL (India) in presence of the then President, ICAI in presence of other dignitaries. More than 1250 delegates from across the Nation has attended this historic Conference. The sessions were taken by the galaxy of star speakers who enriched the participants with an illuminating and educative experience. In the Valedictory Ceremony, Deputy Chief Minister of Odisha was Chief Guest along with CMD of NALCO, were present. • Focus on quality and availability of CPE Programmes across globe and to enhance quality of CPE Programmes in terms of CPE Learning & Delivery Mechanism To enhance the quality and relevance of CPE programmes, the CPE Committee of ICAI (CPEC) has strengthened its regulatory framework with improved topic selection, faculty engagement, and programme structuring. A stricter monitoring system ensures compliance by CPE Organising Units (POUs), promoting accountability and continual improvement. CPEC has also introduced a faculty review and feedback mechanism to assess teaching effectiveness and build a strong pool of competent faculty. The CPE Portal now features an advanced faculty search tool for better faculty selection. Additionally, CPEC provides financial support to Regional Councils, Branches, and Study Chapters to promote accessible, high-quality learning across India. • - CPE Programme - To reach out to the Member of ICAI at remotest place of country for dissemination of knowledge With the objective of promoting the ICAI brand, the CPE Committee has undertaken a new initiative this year aimed at reaching even the remotest members of ICAI across the country for effective dissemination of knowledge. This initiative has been themed as '- – CPE Programme' and it ensures for the effective transmission of knowledge to every ICAI member, regardless of their location. • New format of CPE Calendar of topics for 2025-26 The CPE Committee revises the CPE Calendar of topics every year for organizing CPE Programmes through its CPE POUs. This year, the Chairman of the CPE Committee has proposed a revamp of the CPE Calendar, suggesting a new format and a more visually appealing design to enhance its presentation. In line with above, this year, the CPE Calendar has been featured in three comprehensive parts i.e Part A outlines standard programme structures focused on current thrust areas within the professional landscape. Part B offers a modulewise learning framework, complete with defined CPE hours and Part C presents an extensive list of suggested topics relevant to Chartered Accountancy professionals. • Organizing of "Train the Trainers" and "Faculty Development Programmes" for development of faculty database by CPE Committee and to have their regular monitoring and review system With the objective of building a nationwide pool of skilled and effective faculty members, the CPE Committee has initiated and proposed "Train the Trainer" programmes across the country. These programmes are designed to equip potential and existing faculty members with the necessary tools and techniques to deliver high-quality learning experiences during CPE sessions. The "Train the Trainer" initiative serves as a strategic approach to enhance the pedagogical capabilities of subject matter experts. It focuses on key areas such as effective teaching methodologies, fostering inclusive and engaging learning environments, improving communication and presentation skills, and mastering the art of public speaking. Through this initiative, the CPE Committee aims to ensure that faculties are not only experts in their subject areas but are also proficient in delivering knowledge in a professional, impactful, and learner-centric manner. • Identification of new CPE POUs to be opened in all Districts/Tehsils in India to connect and facilitate the members in mofussil/remote areas to undergo CPE activities The CPE Committee as per its Action Plan 2025-26 proposed to reach to 1000+ POUs in all States/Districts/Tehsils (Mofussil Areas) in India to facilitate CPE learning to all members of the ICAI residing in far flung areas of the country. The Committee is keen in expanding its strong Network base of 696 POUs vide opening of CPE POUs in all Districts/Tehsils in India to Connect and facilitate the members in mofussil/remote areas to undergo CPE activities. With this aim, the CPE Committee proposed to open CPE POUs in all States/Districts/Tehsils in India to facilitate all members of the ICAI residing in far flung areas of the country for organisation of CPE programmes and for helping/aiding the members in mofussil/remote areas to undergo CPE activities in their jurisdiction areas. In this regard, the Committee researched on the data of ICAI Members and identified the possibility of opening new CPE Study Chapters & Study Groups as CPE POUs in all Districts/Tehsils in India and accordingly informed and invited all the ICAI Members District-wise with a request to identify and inform the other areas where CPE POUs (CPE Study Chapters and Study Groups) may be opened. • Members feedback on CPE Mechanism and implementation Recognizing the importance of Continuous Professional Education (CPE) in a rapidly changing environment, the CPE Committee is committed to supporting members' professional growth. To improve CPE guidelines and delivery, an online feedback form has been introduced to gather members' experiences, preferences, and suggestions. This input will help tailor the CPE framework to better meet members' needs and enhance the overall effectiveness of the program. • Providing Opportunities for CA Professionals - • To participate as a Speaker / Faculty/ Moderator in the CPEC Webinars CPE Committee is committed to provide sufficient opportunities to Members by providing suitable platform to act as a Speaker / Faculty/ Moderator. Taking it forward, during 2024–25, the Webinars were held every Wednesday and Friday, offering opportunities to approximately 400 members to participate as Speakers, Faculty, or Moderators in the CPEC Webinars. • Introduction of Webinar series on 'Opportunities for CAs in emerging areas' in the year 2025 :

This year, the Committee has taken an initiative by introducing three Webinar series for the benefit of CA Professionals, on following emerging areas i.e :- Opportunities for CA Professionals in Foreign Country, in specific Industries (Sector wise) and in Government Schemes (Sector wise) • Re-constitution of CPE Regional Monitoring Committee (RMC) in all five Regions for the Council year 2025-26 For 2025-26, the CPE Committee has reconstituted Regional Monitoring Committees (RMCs) in all five regions to oversee CPE activities. Their responsibilities include reviewing monthly reports and attendance on the CPE Portal, addressing issues faced by CPE units, monitoring the functioning of study chapters and circles, nominating monitors, collecting and compiling participant feedback, and submitting quarterly reports. RMCs are also required to meet at least once every quarter and report their proceedings to the CPE Committee.

(II) IT Initiatives undertaken by the CPE Committee for Members & CPE Programme Organising Units (CPE POUs) • Automation of CPE Portal and development of Mobile Application for CPE Activities and attendance The CPE Portal is a comprehensive platform managing ICAI's CPE activities, awarding Structured and Unstructured CPE Hours. The CPE Committee is enhancing the portal with advanced IT features like a redesigned interface, single sign-in, real-time attendance tracking, and improved faculty management. Upgrades also include member and POU dashboards, WhatsApp notifications for CPE status, and integration with the ICAI digital learning hub, aiming to improve user experience, transparency, and accessibility while serving as a knowledge hub for members.

Initiative towards Partner in Nation Building • Connecting with Members:To enhance outreach and accessibility, the CPE Committee has established 17 new CPE Programme Organising Units (POUs), bringing the total to 696 across India and abroad. This network, including ICAI branches, foreign chapters, committees, study circles, and chapters, plays a key role in delivering structured CPE programmes, especially in remote areas. The CPE Committee is also encouraging members to identify and establish new POUs in underserved regions, promoting equitable and inclusive access to professional development for all ICAI members. • Knowledge dissemination:• Seminars/ Webinars/Workshops/RRCs:

To accelerate the process of continuous learning and professional development, the Continuing Professional Education Committee (CPEC) has been proactively organizing an increasing number of programmes on topics of current and emerging professional interest. These programmes are being conducted across various parts of the country and abroad, ensuring wide geographic coverage and accessibility. The formats include National Conferences, Seminars, Workshops, Webinars and Residential Refresher Courses. These initiatives reflect the Committee's commitment to equipping members with updated knowledge, practical insights, and technical skills required to stay updated in a rapidly evolving professional landscape. • Strengthening the Branding & enhancement of Capacity building of Members through continuous learning process:

For skill upgradation and to update the knowledge of members, CPE Committee is organising programmes on traditional and non-traditional areas on regular basis. In addition to imparting training on technical topics, the Committee is also focusing on updating public speaking skills of members and mentoring them to become thought leader. The programmes being organised revolves around: Disseminating knowledge on the theory and practice on various Technical and current professional topics. Enhancing role of members in diversified areas of profession. Providing networking opportunity to members. Members were given opportunity to come forward and they were given chance to become faculty/Moderator in the CPE Committee programmes. • Details of Interactive meet organised by CPEC: ➢ Exclusively with the Convenors & Deputy Convenors of CPE Study Circles, CPE Study Chapters & CPE Study Groups and with the Chairmen and Managing Committee members of Regional Councils and Branches The CPE Committee has organised various interactive meetings with the Chairmen & Vice-Chairmen of Regional Councils & Branches and also with the Convenors & Dy. Convenors of all CPE Study Circles, CPE Study Chapters and CPE Study Groups, Region-wise in Online mode, seeking suggestions for further improvement in the Continuing Professional Development policies and programmes, to educate them about various policies, guidelines and initiatives of CPE Committee as well as to understand expectation/to get feedback of CPE POUs for improvisation of CPE Programmes etc. The CPE Committee has organized 4 Interactive meetings in online mode. ➢ All India Managing Committee Members Meet (AIMCMM) 2025 organised jointly by RBA Directorate & CPE Committee from 21st to 23rd March, 2025 The All India Management Committee Members Meet (AIMCMM)–2025 was held from 21st to 23rd March in New Delhi, jointly organized by the CPE Committee and RBA Directorate. Over 250 Convenors and Dy.

Convenors of CPE Study Circles and Chapters attended the event, which focused on the 2025–26 Action Plan, CPE policies, guidelines, and recent initiatives. The meet aimed to gather feedback for improving CPE programmes, enhancing member compliance, and promoting best practices. Themed "VISHWASNIYA", the event served as a platform for orientation, strategic planning, and knowledge sharing.

(III) Major Events undertaken directly by CPE Committee:• Online CPE Learning eventsThe CPE Committee of ICAI regularly organizes online CPE events to promote knowledge sharing and continuous professional development. These virtual sessions keep members updated on emerging trends, regulatory changes, and best practices, while also contributing to Unstructured CPE hours. The initiative supports lifelong learning and helps members stay aligned with the evolving professional landscape. • National Conferences/ Residential Refresher Course (RRC)/ Webinars and Seminars/Workshops:The CPE Committee organised 12 National Conference on Indirect Taxes & Direct Taxes, Capital Market, AI and Income Tax, GST & Its Litigation, Company Audit & Its Financial Statements and Artificial Intelligence etc. The CPE Committee organised 9 Residential Refresher Course (RRC) on Recent Issues in Trust, Overview of Valuation and Notices u/s 148 & 148A, Income Tax and GST, Aggregation & Merger of Audit Firms, Code of Ethics and SME IPO etc. The Committee organised various Webinars, Seminars and Workshops.

7.8 Professional Development Committee (PDC) The Professional Development Committee was established in 1962 with a mission to enhance skill sets of the members of the Institute in the existing and new areas.

(I) MEETINGS WITH VARIOUS REGULATORS AND OTHER OUTSIDER BODIES:

The PDC in its efforts to explore the unchartered areas for professional opportunities interact with the Government, regulatory authorities etc. requesting them to avail the expertise of the Chartered Accountants and utilize their services in various areas. • Meetings held with Audit Relation Group, Department of Supervision, RBI : ➢ Meeting with Audit Relation Group, RBI, Mumbai on 3rd June, 2024 at Mumbai to discuss various issues concerning the audit profession in the banking industry including introduction of additional criteria for categorization of firms, Reintroduction of cooling period in Public Sector Banks, Increase in the audit fee for Statutory Central and Branch Audit assignments, Increase in Coverage of Advances vis-à-vis Concurrent Audit System etc. ➢ Meeting with the Audit relation group of the RBI on 8th October, 2024 wherein concerns raised by members with respect to a situation where the partnership firm looses the opportunity to get empaneled with RBI owing to the fact that it has only one partner being exclusively associated with the firm were taken up by ICAI. Apart from the same, various other issues concerning the audit profession in the banking industry were discussed. ➢ Meeting with RBI on 14th February, 2025 on 'Coordination of Regional Chapters of ICAI with the Regional Office of RBI on NBFC-Related Matters. ➢ Meeting with Executive Director, RBI on 1st April, 2025 at Mumbai to discuss matters relating audit of regulated entities of RBI including appointment of SCAs and SBAs, Coverage of advance and Branches under audit, Policy of rotation as well as cooling period in Public Sector Banks, Discretion proposed to be provided to the Public Sector Banks in the matter of remuneration of auditors and among others. ➢ Meeting with Executive Director, RBI on 8th April, 2025 at Delhi to discuss various matters pertaining to Governance Framework of Audit in Public Sector Banks. ➢ Meeting with CGM, Department of Supervision, RBI on 29.05.2025 at Mumbai to discuss various matters concerning the audit profession in the banking industry including structured training programme for Audit Committee Chairman of Board of Banking Industry, proposal of regular meetings between RBI and ICAI, awareness about NBFC compliances etc. • Various meetings were held with the O/o Central Registrar of Cooperative Societies, Ministry of Cooperation ➢ Meeting with Joint Secretary and Central Registrar of Cooperative Societies, Ministry of Cooperation on 16th July, 2024 wherein various concerns relating to the panel of auditors for Multi State Cooperative Societies prepared by the Office of Central Registrar of Cooperative Societies were discussed. ➢ Meeting with Joint Central Registrar of Cooperative Societies, Ministry of Cooperation: A meeting was held with Joint Central Registrar of Cooperative Societies, Ministry of Cooperation on 29th July, 2024 wherein issues related to the empanelment of auditors for UCBs and Multi State Cooperative Banks were discussed. ➢ Meeting with Central Registrar of Cooperative Societies, Ministry of Cooperation on 7th October 2024 with Central Registrar of Cooperative Societies, Ministry of Cooperation to discuss various concerns raised by the members with regard to order 3rd October, 2024, issued by Office of Central Registrar; notifying the panels of auditors for the FY 2024-25. ➢ Meeting with Central Registrar of Cooperative Societies, Ministry of Co-operation on 16.12.2024 for discussing strengthening of audit mechanism, Standards/Norms of Auditing and Accounting and panel of Auditors in the Multi-State Cooperative Societies. ➢ Meeting with the Central Registrar of Cooperative Societies, Ministry of Cooperation on 19th February, to discuss ICAI's suggestions on the guidelines on the empanelment for the appointment/reappointment of Statutory and Concurrent Auditors of Multi-State Cooperative Societies, including Multi-State Cooperative Banks. ➢ Meeting with CRCS, Ministry of Cooperation on 30.04.2025 to discuss the challenges faced by audit firms, particularly with respect to Concurrent Audit assignments. ➢ Meeting of the Committee constituted by the Ministry of Cooperation on 13th May, 2025 for formulation of Auditing and Accounting norms, standards and Auditors panel for Multistate Co-operative Societies. • Meetings in National Bank for Agriculture and Rural Development (NABARD) ➢ Meeting with CGM, NABARD on 16.04.2024 to discuss various issues pertaining to audit to RRBs and Co-operative Audits and preparation panel in line with Guidelines regarding the appointment/reappointment of Statutory Auditors for State Co-operative Banks and Central Co-operative Banks (StCCB's and CCB). ➢ Meeting with CGM, NABARD on 25.07.2024 to discuss issues related to Statutory Audit of RCBs and RRBs and certain matters of Cooperative audits. • Meeting was held with Deputy Chief Executive, Indian Bank's Association on 15.10.2024 at Mumbai to discuss various aspects of Banking sector vis-a vis the role of Chartered Accountants including recommendations of ICAI on Advisory dated 27th May 2024 issued by IBA in respect of remuneration payable to SCAs and SBAs for various items of audit works; involvement of ICAI in certain aspects of empanelment for Forensic Audit and ASM audit; dissemination of information about the panel of ICAI in respect of "OA" category introduced by ICAI for "Other assignments" in Banks; issues arising due to lack of uniformity in formats of certificates etc. • Meeting was held with Chief Executive, Indian Bank's Association on 29.05.2025 in Mumbai to discuss various matters of Banking sector with respect to the role of Chartered Accountants. • Meeting with the Office of C&AG ➢ Meeting held with Deputy C&AG, DG Commercial-I and DG Commercial-II, O/o C&AG on 26th February, 2024 to consider the issues reported by members due to suspension of internet services owing to the ongoing farmer protests in various regions of Punjab, Haryana, and Uttar Pradesh and outskirts of Delhi & NCR, a representation was submitted to consider opening the online application for Empanelment for Chartered Accountant Firms/LLPs for the year 2024-25 for two days. ➢ Meeting held with DG Commercial-I, O/o C&AG on 12.06.2024 to address various representations with regard to data furnished with O/o C&AG under the head "Branch details, a One-Time online window was opened from 05.07.2024 to 08.07.2024, to update/add/delete the data relating to their branch offices alongwith their members stationed at a particular branch office submitted by the applicants in their Online application filed with O/o C&AG. • Meeting with Department of Posts (DoP) on April 24, 2024 to explore professional opportunities for Chartered accountants in auditing post offices and discuss various matters concerning the accounting and auditing systems including transition from a cash accounting system to an accrual-based accounting system. • Meeting with Department of Financial Services, Ministry of Finance on 7th May, 2024 at New Delhi to discuss the matter related to the revision of audit fees for statutory auditors of Regional Rural Banks (RRBs) and the proposal for an upward revision of the fees for RRBs was submitted under the signatures of President, ICAI. • Meeting with Senior Advisor, Niti Aayog on 7th May, 2024 at New Delhi to discuss on the collaboration between ICAI and NITI Aayog WEP for empowering women entrepreneurs. In this regard, deliberations were made on materializing the MOU between WEP and ICAI. Every month, NITI Aayog conducts the AskWEP series on various topics to provide knowledge to women entrepreneurs. For the sessions to be held, the ICAI as a knowledge partner, is supporting the event by sharing the program structure and providing speakers/ moderator for the relevant topics. • State Level Coordination Committee (SLCC) meetings held during the year : During the year more than 50 SLCC meetings were held by RBI all across India which were attended by the representatives of ICAI.

(II) ACHIEVEMENTS: • Representation to the Hon'ble Finance Minister, Govt of India on the issue of Modification in the Banking Law (Amendment) Bill, 2024 providing discretion to public sector banks in the matter of remuneration of auditors. In this regard, concerns were raised by members of the ICAI opposing the amendment as these changes may inadvertently weaken governance frameworks in public sector banks. • Feedback to the office of C&AG on the policy of empanelment and allotment of chartered accountants: A meeting in this regard was also held with Director General (Commercial-II), Office of Comptroller & Auditor General of India on 03.01.2025. • Submissions made to Audit Relation Group, Department of Supervision, RBI on various matters concerning the audit profession in the banking industry including introduction of additional criteria for categorization of firms, Reintroduction of cooling period in Public Sector Banks, Increase in the audit fee for Statutory Central and Branch Audit assignments, Increase in Coverage of Advances vis-à-vis Concurrent Audit System, Draft Training Programme structure for Audit Committee Chairman of the Board of PSB etc. • Recommendation regarding Guidelines for empanelment O/o Central Registrar of Cooperative Societies, Ministry of Cooperation on 30th January, 2025 with regard to suggestions of ICAI on Appointment / Re-appointment of Statutory/Concurrent Auditors of Multi-State Cooperative societies including Multistate co-operative banks. Apart from the same, at various occasions submissions were made to the Office of CRCS highlighting the concerns raised regarding panel of auditors adopted for Statutory/Concurrent Auditors of Multi-State Cooperative societies including Multistate co-operative banks. • Recommendations to Indian Banks' Association 27th May 2024 issued by IBA in respect of remuneration payable to SCAs and SBAs for various items of audit works was submitted to IBA; • Introduction of additional category apart from Categories of RBII- PDC has introduced a new MEF category, "OA" (Other Assignments), to help small or new CA firms that don't qualify for RBI's statutory audit norms. This category enables them to get non-statutory assignments like concurrent and stock audits. It includes firms or individuals exclusively associated with the applicant as of 1st July. Banks and relevant authorities are being informed to consider these firms for such assignments. • Representation for Reconsideration of Pre-Qualification Criteria to bid in "Invitation for Proposal for Empanelment to take up Assignments Relating to Forensic Audits" issued by Home Department, Government of Maharashtra- The PDC, based on DAAB's recommendation and with the Chairman's approval, submitted a representation to the Home Department, Government of Maharashtra regarding their forensic audit empanelment criteria. It highlighted that Chartered Accountants, through ICAI's FAFD course and FAIS 420 standard, are trained in digital forensics and can take expert assistance when needed.

Therefore, CAs should be allowed to participate in bids for digital forensic assignments.

(IV) OTHER ACTIVITIES:

Various Programmes/Meets organised by the Committee: • An Interactive meet themed as "Celebrating ICAI's 75 years Legacy with Indian Banking SectorPioneering the path for Amrit Kaal in India" held on 19th June, 2024 at Mumbai on the topics of

"Regulatory Compliance and Governance in Banking" and "Automation in the Banking Sector: The Era of Digital Transformation." The meet was attended by more than 40 delegates in person. • Interactive Meet on Statutory Audit of Urban Cooperative Banks with Statutory Auditors and CA Directors, CFO's, Chairman, Vice Chairman and Audit Committee Chairman of Urban Cooperative Banks at Kolhapur (26.08.2024), Nagpur (28.08.2024), and Faridabad (02.09.2024). Discussions were held on important topics like Challenges and critical aspects w.r.t. Compliances with Accounting Standards and Disclosures norms; CRAR / Treasury Audit / Regulatory compliances under BR Act and Co-operative Societies' Act; Statutory Audit Report, LFAR and Certifications and Challenges in system based NPA / IRACP. • Central Statutory Auditors' Meet 2025-26 on 24th March 2025 in Mumbai in Hybrid mode and more than 150 Central Statutory Auditors from Public Sector Banks, Private Sector Banks, and Small Finance Banks, participated in the meeting. A special session was on the "RBI's perspective and Expectations from the Statutory Central Auditors." Discussions during the meeting encompassed several important topics such as Intricacies of Treasury Audit of SCBs in the light of revised Master Directions, Pondering over system based asset classification & Syncing with accounting, Certifications, LFAR, IFCoFR, Independent Auditor's Report by SCA and Usage of CAAT Tools/ Data Analytics. • Diploma and Certificate Courses organised/to be organised by the Committee : ➢ Certificate Course on Project Financing: 2 batches of certificate course have been conducted Duration of course No of members registered Sl.No.
