No. of members registered to the DIRM Course Total Qualified Eligibility Test Qualified Technical Examination Central Region Eastern Region Northern Region Southern Region Western Region 81 (but yet to undergo Orientation Programme) • After seeking approval of the Council to conduct the Orientation Programme in online mode, the Committee conducted Two days DIRM Orientation Programme for members who have passed the DIRM Technical Examination during 27th May – 30th May 2025 through Digital Learning Hub, ICAI. To align the 2 - day DIRM Orientation Programme with current industry trends and professional requirements, the programme structure has been revised with the approval of the Committee. • Webinars, Seminars and Conferences:

PDC commenced a series of Webinars under the name PDC's DNA-Discover New Avenues.

7.9 Professional Skill Enrichment Committee In a first of its kind initiative, the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India has set up the Professionals Skills Enrichment Committee with an aim to enable all Chartered Accountants to realise their complete potential, to Understand requirements, Assess the present skills gap amongst all Chartered accountants and enable actions to enhance their competence and improve their visibility amongst the stakeholders.The Professional Skills Enrichment Committee proposes to organize Skills Enrichment Programmes during the year 2024-25. The key skills proposed to be covered are Branding skills, Communication skills, Critical thinking skills, Design Thinking, Emotional Intelligence, Entrepreneurial Skills, Inter-personal skills, Leadership skills, Listening Skills, Negotiation skills, Networking skills, Problem Solving skills, Public speaking skills, Team Building skills, Time Management, Work Ethics any other skill and new age, contemporary & other professional, technical and technological skills.

The aforesaid programmes emphasis is on enriching skill sets of the Chartered Accountants.

Initiatives of the Professional Skills Enrichment Committee (PSEC), ICAI • Professional Skills Enrichment Committee, ICAI Awarded with the India Book of Records(IBR) & Asia Book of Records(ABR) for achieving various milestones ➢ Professional Skills Enrichment Committee, ICAI has been honoured by the India Book of Records (IBR) and Asia Book of Records (ABR) for conducting 97 programs on the record title of "Maximum Programs Conducted on Entrepreneurship Skills in a Day" across branches of Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (ICAI). ➢ Professional Skills Enrichment Committee, ICAI has been recognized by India Book of Records (IBR) and Asia Book of Records (ABR) for conducting 79 programs on the record title "Maximum Skill Enrichment Programs Conducted in a Fortnight" across branches of Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (ICAI). • An Interactive Virtual Workshop Series focused on Skills Enrichment for the Members of ICAI: The ICAI Workshop Series is a structured platform encouraging members to actively participate and share insights. Conducted three times a week for 1.5 hours each, it focuses on enhancing professional and soft skills such as public speaking and communication. Led by expert faculties, these interactive sessions aim to equip Chartered Accountants with essential tools and techniques, promote continuous learning, and foster peer networking in a supportive environment. • Webcast Series on various aspects of Skills Enrichment of Chartered Accountants: The webcast series focuses on Skills Enrichment for Chartered Accountants, offering in-depth sessions every Saturday, with five sessions monthly, including special programs during World Youth Skills Day and Skills Enrichment month in July and August 2024. Each session provides 2 CPE hours and covers a wide range of technical and soft skills like financial planning and leadership development. Delivered by expert speakers, the series offers a flexible platform for continuous learning, helping participants stay updated on industry trends, build new competencies, and engage in interactive discussions to support career advancement. • Enriching Entrepreneur Skills Program for Chartered Accountants: Around 100 physical programs on entrepreneurial skills were conducted on June 26-27, 2024, aimed at equipping Chartered Accountants with practical insights and strategies for business innovation and leadership. Designed for CAs aspiring to enhance their entrepreneurial capabilities, the programs covered strategic planning, financial management, innovation, and leadership. Through interactive workshops, case studies, and mentorship, participants gained valuable tools to navigate business challenges confidently. This initiative reflects a strong commitment to fostering innovation and entrepreneurship among CAs, empowering them to become successful business leaders beyond their financial expertise. • World Youth Skills Day (Celebrated on 15th July 2024): World Youth Skills Month is an initiative dedicated to celebrating and promoting youth skill development, preparing the next generation of professionals for success in a rapidly changing world. It emphasizes the vital role of skill-building in empowering young people, driving economic growth, and strengthening communities. The day unites governments, educational institutions, businesses, and youth to share best practices and create opportunities for skill enhancement, ensuring young individuals gain the competencies needed for sustainable livelihoods.

Investing in youth skills today secures a future of innovation, leadership, and positive societal impact. • Skills Enrichment Month: August 2024 is designated as Skills Enrichment Month, focused on enhancing the professional and personal capabilities of individuals across various fields. Throughout the month, targeted workshops, training sessions, and seminars will help participants develop new skills, sharpen existing ones, and stay updated on industry trends. The initiative promotes a culture of continuous learning, empowering professionals to excel and adapt to the evolving global workforce by providing tools and resources for lifelong growth in technical skills, communication, leadership, and more. • Daily Virtual Yoga Session for Chartered Accountants: A new initiative offering Virtual Yoga Sessions for Chartered Accountants aims to enhance well-being and productivity by reducing stress, improving flexibility and strength, boosting immunity, and promoting work-life balance. Suitable for all levels, these sessions run Monday to Friday from 7 to 7:45 a.m., and weekends from 9 to 9:45 a.m., in partnership with Bharatiya Yog Sansthan. Approximately 199 sessions will be conducted until February 11, 2025. • Daily Virtual Programme on Work life Balance for Chartered Accountants: The committee launched a Work-Life Balance initiative to help professionals maintain harmony between their work and personal lives, promoting mental and physical health, reducing stress, and enhancing overall happiness. The program offers daily virtual sessions Monday to Friday (8:30–9:30 p.m.) and weekend sessions (10:30–11:30 a.m.) in partnership with Heartfulness, with around 100 sessions planned until February 11, 2025. • Social media presence : The Committee actively maintain a strong social media presence to connect with audience, build brand awareness, and drive growth. By sharing insightful updates, engaging in industry discussions, and using visual storytelling, we enhance visibility and foster relationships. Leveraging analytics helps us tailor content to audience preferences, ensuring our efforts remain effective and aligned with our goals. A vibrant social media strategy is essential for meaningful connections and sustained success in today's digital landscape. • Exclusive Website: Exclusive Website of the Professional Skills Enrichment Committee, ICAI https://psec.icai.org/ focusing on enhancing user experience, accessibility, and functionality. As digital presence becomes increasingly crucial, the goal is to create a platform that is not only visually appealing but also user-friendly and responsive across all devices, to integrate advanced features such as interactive content, streamlined navigation, and robust security measures to ensure that our website meets the needs of our audience • Publications of the Committee: The Committee publishes a series of books covering key professional skills for Chartered Accountants, including work-life balance, active listening, critical thinking, emotional intelligence, public speaking, negotiation, and entrepreneurship. These publications reflect extensive research and collaboration, offering valuable insights and practical guidance to support continuous learning and development. Meticulously crafted, the books serve as trusted resources that advance knowledge and foster growth within the CA community. • Launching of the Certificate Course on Skill Enrichment: The Certificate course on skill enrichment demonstrates a proactive approach to personal and professional development. This course comprises Communication Skills, Interpersonal Skills, Leadership and Teamwork, Stress Management and Work – life Balance, Professional Ethics and Integrity, Thinking Skills and Technology, Branding and Networking , Practical Application and case studies and New age & Contemporary Skills . • Seminars/Workshops/Conferences (Physical Mode) : The Committee has conducted 208 Skill Enrichment seminars across ICAI branches up to February 2025, offering valuable learning opportunities for members to enhance professional competencies. These expert-led sessions cover leadership, communication, technical skills, and personal development, providing interactive and practical experiences.

The seminars support continuous growth, boost career prospects, confidence, and networking among professionals and students. • Webcast Series on various aspects of Skills Enrichment of Chartered Accountants: This webcast series takes a deep dive into the Skills Enrichment of Chartered Accountants. Throughout the webcast series, we have taken a deep dive into the various aspects of by discussing a broad range of Skills, aiming to enhance the portfolio of Chartered Accountants. As on 07.02.2025, 87 Webcasts on various aspects of Skills Enrichment organized for the Members of ICAI.

7.10 Women & Members Excellence Committee (WMEC) As a true partner in nation building, ICAI has setup the Women & Young Members Excellence Committee (erstwhile Women Members Excellence Committee) which is a non-standing Committee of ICAI formed under regulatory provisions of Chartered Accountants Act, 1949. With the inclusion of Young members within the ambit of the Committee, the Committee has widened its scope to effectively work towards excellence and upskilling of both women & young member in the dynamic and challenging work environment. WYMEC especially work towards promoting the fulfilment of Women and young Members' potential through capacity building initiatives, skill development activities, providing platform for professional and employment opportunities.

Activities

(I) Placement Programme for Flexi time/ Part time/ Work from Home Job Opportunities for Women Chartered Accountants- The Committee, jointly with the Committee for Members in Industry & Business

(CMIB), conducted placement programme specifically designed for its women members, to explore more placement opportunities for those women members who want to work on a flexi-time/ part-time/ work from home basis. This placement programme aimed to provide valuable opportunities for women CAs and have received positive response from both recruiters and members.

(II) 2nd CA Women Excellence Awards- The Committee organized the 2nd CA Women Excellence Awards on 1st February 2025 at Yashobhoomi, New Delhi, to honor women members excelling in leadership roles across corporates, organizations, practice, and social contributions. Awards were presented in five categories: CA Woman Lifetime Achievement, CA Woman of the Year, CA Woman Startup, CA Woman Social Services, and CA Woman Independent Director. The ceremony, held alongside the World Forum of Accountants, was graced by Minister of Law and Justice Chief Guest, presenting the trophies to the winners.

(III) CA Women Leadership Conclave "Falcon"- The Committee, aligned with the Government's Women-Led Development agenda, successfully organized the CA Women Leadership Conclave "Falcon" on 29th June 2024 at Mumbai, bringing together over 500 women Chartered Accountants. The event featured sessions on investing, professional opportunities, well-being, and leadership, delivered by eminent women professionals and industry leaders. Building on this success, the Committee hosted a series of follow-up Falcon events across various branches between August and December 2024, covering topics such as novel professional opportunities, leadership, AI and office automation, women independent directors, financial management, mental wellness, and presentation skills. These events provided valuable platforms for knowledge exchange, inspiration, and networking among women CAs, fostering professional growth and empowerment.

(IV) Relaunch of the Women Portal for Flexi & Part-time Work options- The Committee relaunched Women Portal https://wmec.icai.org/ for Flexi & Part-time work options. This portal enables both employers and job seekers to register, facilitating better job matching and networking. A key feature of the portal is its API integration with the SSP portal, allowing job seekers to complete forms quickly by capturing necessary data from the SSP portal, thus reducing the time required for registration. More than 600 employers and more than 9000 women members registered on the portal.

(V) Independent Directors' Conclave- The Committee successfully organised the first Independent Directors' Conclave on 6th June 2024 in Mumbai and the second series of Independent Directors' Conclave on 20th June 2024 in Ahmedabad on the theme- "Shaping the future of effective Corporate Governance", a subject paramount to the integrity and sustainability of our corporate landscape. These Conclaves aimed to empower and encourage members to pursue careers as Independent Director, foster their professional growth and enrich their skill set through targeted technical training. More than 150 Chartered Accountants participated in each of the Conclave.

(VI) Publication on "The Future is Female and it's now"- The Committee released a new publication titled "The Future is Female and It's Now," highlighting various facets of women leadership. It covers topics such as the role of women in Indian philosophy and civilization, women leaders in India, leadership across social, political, and corporate sectors, strategies to increase women leadership, barriers faced globally and in India, and the rise of young women leaders in the country.

(VII) Residential Refresher Courses (RRCs)- The Committee organized three Residential Refresher Courses for women members focusing on skill-building, networking, and professional growth. These courses, held at Jaipur, Jhansi, and Mahabalipuram, covered topics such as new opportunities for CAs, tax laws, ethics, leadership, audit in the digital age, financial crimes, and work-life balance.

(VIII) International Women's Day Activity- To commemorate International Women's Day on 8th March 2025, the Women & Young Members Excellence Committee undertook several initiatives to inspire and engage women members and students of ICAI. Video bytes from ICAI's President, Vice President, Chairperson, and Vice-Chairman of WYMEC were recorded and shared across social media and the Committee's platforms. The Committee also sent mass SMS greetings to women members and circulated informative social media creatives highlighting ICAI's efforts towards women's excellence. Additionally, over 95 ICAI branches nationwide organized Women Empowerment programs under WYMEC's guidance, engaging around 5,000 members in various celebratory events.

(IX) Physical programs/events hosted by Branches- The Committee conducted six physical programs across ICAI branches in 2024, focusing on women's professional growth, leadership, work-life balance, legal awareness, and emerging opportunities for women Chartered Accountants.

(X) - An IDOL- Inclusivity | Diversity | Outstanding | Leadership Webinar Series- The Committee organized 59 webinars under the " – An IDOL" series, featuring expert speakers on technical and non-technical topics. This initiative aimed to update members, enhance their skills, and empower women to showcase leadership and communication by coordinating the sessions.

(XI) SAFA Women Leadership Awards 2025- To recognize the accomplishments of enterprising women who are affiliated with the Accountancy Profession in SAARC region, the SAFA Women Leadership Committee had invited nominations for the 2nd SAFA Women Leadership Awards. To promote this initiative, the Committee proactively circulated the information regarding the SAFA Women Leadership Awards amongst the Women members of ICAI through mass email and SMS communications.

8. OTHER COMMITTEES/BOARDS

8.1 Audit Committee The Audit Committee of the Institute was established in April 2001 by the Council as a non-standing committee with a clearly defined mandate. Since 2009, it has been chaired exclusively by a Government Nominee to ensure greater independence. To further enhance its autonomy, members of the Executive Committee and Finance Committee are not permitted to serve on the Central Audit Committee. Additionally, five Regional Audit Committees—Southern, Western, Central, Northern, and Eastern—support the Committee's work, ensuring regional representation and effective oversight.

Functions The Committee is responsible for overseeing the financial reporting process and ensuring the accuracy, integrity, and transparency of the Institute's financial disclosures. It reviews audit reports, discusses observations with auditors, recommends corrective actions, and strives to strengthen the internal control environment. The Committee remains committed to good governance and continuous improvement in audit practices.

Statutory Audit • Appointment of Statutory Auditors- In accordance with Section 18(5) of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, the statutory auditors for ICAI's Branches, Regional Councils, Decentralised Offices, Centre of Excellence, and Head Office are appointed from a panel of two firms recommended by the Comptroller and Auditor General (C&AG) of India, effective from FY 2022–23. This system ensures continuity and avoids audit delays. A status report on these appointments is regularly shared with the C&AG. • Audit Reporting Framework- The statutory auditors' reporting framework is governed by Regulations 196 and 197 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1988. • Joint Statutory Auditors- The accounts of the ICAI Head Office are audited by two joint statutory auditors, bringing diverse expertise and ensuring comprehensive review and accountability.

Internal Audit • Internal Audit Coverage- A robust internal audit mechanism is in place for all major units including branches with over a thousand members, Regional Councils, Decentralised Offices, the Centre of Excellence and the Head Office. The internal audit is conducted under the supervision of the Audit Committee, which ensures transparency and auditor independence. • Appointment through EoI- Internal auditors are appointed through a transparent process involving an open invitation for Expressions of Interest (EoI) from CA firms. Selection is based on an objective evaluation and ranking system. Audits are conducted biannually and cover both financial and compliance aspects, as per the prescribed scope and reporting format. Audit reports are reviewed by the respective Regional or Central Audit Committees for follow-up action.

Annual Accounts • Unmodified Audit Opinions- The Institute has consistently received unmodified (unqualified) audit opinions, reflecting sound financial management, robust internal controls, and adherence to high governance standards. • Financial Statement Format- ICAI prepares its financial statements in accordance with the illustrative format for Not-for-Profit Organizations, as per the Technical Guide issued by the Institute. The format is customized to enhance relevance and clarity for stakeholders. • Approval Process- The approval of audited financial statements follows a structured and statutory process under Section 18(5A) of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, and Regulation 200 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1988: ➢ Draft accounts are reviewed by the Finance Committee and submitted to the Management Committee. ➢ The Management Committee evaluates and forwards them to the Audit Committee. ➢ The Audit Committee reviews and submits the accounts to the Council. ➢ The Council sends the accounts to the statutory auditors. ➢ Post-audit, the report is circulated among Council members. ➢ The Council finally approves the accounts and ensures their publication in the Gazette of India.

8.2 Committee for Members in Entrepreneurship and Public Services (CME&PS) The Committee for Members in Entrepreneurship and Public Service (CMEPS) acts as an important link between ICAI leadership and members engaged in public service or entrepreneurship. With a focus on promoting the Public Service and Entrepreneurship as career option for CAs, the Committee undertakes various initiatives to inspire and empower members. Many Chartered Accountants currently serve in senior roles across government departments, ministries, legislatures, judiciary, regulatory bodies, and entrepreneurial ventures and actively contributing to governance, economic growth, and the nation's development. The Committee organizes Residential Meets/ Interactive Meets of ICAI members in Public Service with the objective of soliciting suggestions of ICAI Members in Public Service to identify matters of National importance and Committee is continuously exploring the various opportunities that lies in Entrepreneurship for its CA members. To encourage aspiring CAs in both fields, the Committee regularly conducts mentorship sessions, orientation and training programmes, webinars, seminars, and regional meets. These initiatives aim to offer guidance, build capacity, and develop an entrepreneurial culture while supporting members in contributing to nation building.

8.3 Committee on Financial Markets and Investors' Protection (CFM&IP) The Committee on Financial Markets and Investors' Protection provide suggestions on various Bills, Notifications, Circulars, Consultation papers and other documents related to Financial Markets to the Government and Regulators. Additionally, the Committee regularly interacts with Ministry of Corporate Affairs, Reserve Bank of India, Securities and Exchange Board of India, Insurance Regulatory and Development Authority, Non- Finance companies (Department of Non-Banking Finance companies of RBI), Forward Markets Commission and Stock Exchanges on the issues relating to Financial Markets, the role of Chartered Accountants and Investors' Protection.

(I) Partner in Nation Building To create awareness amongst the public at large and to ensure Investors' Protection, the Committee conducted 177 Investor Awareness Programmes through various Programme Organizing Units of ICAI i.e. Branches/Regional Councils/Study Circles, to enhance the ability of investors to understand broad developments of the Financial Markets under the aegis of Investor Education and Protection Fund Authority (IEPFA) of the Ministry of Corporate Affairs (MCA).

on facilitating investments by Indian Mutual Funds in such overseas funds that invest certain portion of their assets in Indian securities dated 17th May 2025 • Consultation Paper on the recommendations of the Expert Committee for facilitating Ease of Doing Business under MB Regulations, BTI Regulations and Buyback Regulations dated 21st May 2024. • Consultation Paper on the (III) Initiatives for the Members • Certificate Courses To enhance the skills of our members and to empower them suitably, the Committee conducts a 6 Certificate Courses which are as follows: ➢ Certificate Course on Forex and Treasury Management (FXTM) ➢ Certificate Course on Derivatives ➢ Certificate Course on Fundamental & Technical Analysis of Stocks including Equity Research ➢ Certificate Course on Financial Markets and Securities Laws (FMSL) ➢ Certificate Course on Compliance of Capital Markets Intermediaries ➢ Certificate course on Fund Management at IFSC The Committee has successfully conducted 10 Online batches through Digital Learning Hub of the Certificate Course on Forex & Treasury Management, Certificate course on Derivatives, Certificate Course on Financial Markets & Securities laws, Certificate course on Fundamental & Technical Analysis of Stocks including Equity Research, Certificate Course on Compliance of Capital Markets Intermediaries and Certificate course on Fund Management at IFSC wherein a total of 819 Members of ICAI have enrolled. • National Conferences/Seminars/Workshop/Webcast/ Residential Refresher Course (RRC) for professional enhancement of members The Committee has disseminated knowledge among members by way of 30 Virtual CPE Meetings and webinars. The Committee on Financial Markets and Investor's Protection conducted 10 National Conferences and 24 Seminars including "Investment ki Pathshala – CA: The Investment Guru". with support of Branches of ICAI.

8.4 Committee on Public and Government Financial Management (CP&GFM) The ICAI in line with its mission and vision, constituted the Committee on Public & Government Financial Management (CPGFM) to improve accounting, financial reporting and management in all tiers of Government in India including local bodies. Apart from formulating Accounting Standards for Local Bodies (ASLBs), the Committee focuses on capacity building of the accounts/ finance staff of various tiers of Government by several means such as Training Programmes, Webinars, e-learning modules, etc. The Committee also runs Certificate Course on Public Finance & Government Accounting that can be attended by officials of Government & Autonomous bodies. In addition, CPGFM provides technical and administrative support for the Certificate Course for Accountants of Panchayats and Municipal Bodies (i.e., a joint initiative of ICAI and O/o C&AG of India) being run through Board for Local Bodies Accountants Certification (BLoAC) established under ICAI Accounting Research Foundation (ICAI-ARF). This initiative reflects ICAI's commitment to nation building by extending the professional expertise of Chartered Accountants beyond the corporate sector to the public at large.

Important activities

(I) Technical Comments Submitted on the drafts of International Public Sector Accounting Standards Board (IPSASB) • Exposure Draft 86, Proposed International Public Sector Accounting Standard on Exploration for and Evaluation of Mineral Resources; • Exposure Draft 87, Proposed International Sector Accounting Standard on Stripping Costs in the Production Phase of a Surface Mine (Amendments to IPSAS 12); • Exposure Draft 88, Proposed International Public Sector Accounting Standard on Arrangements Conveying Rights over Assets (Amendments to IPSAS 47 & IPSAS 48); • Exposure Draft 89, 'Amendments to Consider IFRIC Interpretations'; • Exposure Draft 90, Amendments to IPSAS as a Result of the Application of IPSAS 46, Measurement; • Exposure Draft 91, Limited-scope Updates to First-time Adoption of Accrual Basis International Public Sector Accounting Standards (IPSAS) (Amendments to IPSAS 33); • Exposure Draft 92, Proposed International Public Sector Accounting Standard, Tangible Natural Resources; and • IPSASB SRS Exposure Draft 1, Climate-Related Disclosures.

(II) Support to the Government: • MoU entered with Office of the Comptroller & Auditor General (C&AG) of India A MoU was signed between ICAI and the O/o C&AG of India on 21st April 2025 to enhance capacity building in public sector audits. Under this, the first Residential Capacity Building Programme for 50 CAG officers was held from 5th May 2025 to 5th June, 2025 at ICAI's Centre of Excellence, Hyderabad, The programme was supported by ICAI's ASB, AASB, and DAAB, administered by ICAI ARF, and coordinated by CPGFM. The inaugural session was attended by senior CAG officials and ICAI Central Council Members. • Technical Comments Submitted on the drafts of GASAB documents/ draft Standards: ➢ Comments submitted on Guidance Note on Accounting of Fixed Assets; and ➢ Drafted a Concept paper on Accounting of Public-Private Partnership Arrangements as desired by O/o GASAB. • Certificate Course for Accountants of Panchayats & Municipal Bodies In pursuance of a MoU signed with the O/o C&AG of India, the Certificate Courses for Accountants of Panchayats and Municipal Bodies were formally launched on 16th November 2023 (Audit Diwas) and the registrations of these courses were initiated on 1st December 2023 through a dedicated website https://lba.icaiarf.org.in/. The CPGFM of ICAI provides required technical & administrative support for these courses and also coordinating with O/o CAG of India. The registrations received till date are as follows:

Particulars 1st Batch 2nd Batch 3rd Batch 4th Batch (till date) Grand Total Certificate Course for Accountants of Panchayats Certificate Course for Accountants of Municipal Bodies Total The certificate course includes two offline exams: a screening exam covering basic accounting and a main exam focused on panchayat/municipal body-specific accounting. ICAI's robust exam system has been adapted for this course, with the Exam Department handling paper setting, evaluation, and results, while BLoAC manages other tasks under its guidance. (III) Mega Event - Local Governance Synergy Conclave organised on 1st & 2nd October 2024 ICAI, through CPGFM and hosted by NIRC, organized a 1.5-day Conclave on 1st –2nd October 2024 in New Delhi to promote its Certificate Courses for Accountants of Panchayats and Municipal Bodies. The event was inaugurated by the then Hon'ble CAG of India, and included felicitation of course toppers. Esteemed guests from MoPR, IIPA, SEBI, and other key institutions attended the event. Around 400 participants were present physically, and 2,000 joined via live webcast, representing various national and international bodies and government departments.

8.5 Editorial Board The Editorial Board is a specialized non-standing committee of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India

(ICAI) dedicated to the mission of consistently providing members with professional knowledge and relevant matters through the journal, The Chartered Accountant. This journal empowers members by enhancing their professional knowledge and enjoys immense value among its readers, with a circulation exceeding 400,000, including both e-journal and printed copies."The Chartered Accountant" journal is a flagship publication of ICAI, showcasing the Institute's thought leadership and strengthening its brand presence among members, students, and stakeholders. Curated by the Editorial Board, the journal serves as a source of insights—delivering cutting-edge developments, expert perspectives, and emerging trends in the accounting profession. With a focus on continuous learning and professional excellence, it plays a vital role in connecting the ICAI community with the evolving landscape of finance, governance, and global best practices.

Initiatives towards Developing Nation Through the monthly journal The Chartered Accountant, the Editorial Board has played a pivotal role in nationbuilding by empowering ICAI members with cutting-edge knowledge, global perspectives, and evolving professional practices. By consistently introducing evolving and contemporary trends and advancing thought leadership, the Board is deeply committed to shaping a future-ready accounting profession that contributes meaningfully to India's economic growth and global standing.

Several significant initiatives in this direction include the following:

(I) Knowledge Enrichment through Articles:

During the period, The Chartered Accountant journal featured a wide array of articles covering diverse and contemporary topics relevant to the accounting profession. In addition to its regular sections of professional interest, the journal published special issues aimed at enriching members' knowledge on critical and emerging subjects. These included focused editions on India – Global Hub for Accountancy Profession, Leadership, Public Finance, Artificial Intelligence, MSMEs, Sustainability, and other key technical domains, to keep its members well-informed and future-ready.

Members/Students Initiatives The Editorial Board continues to play a proactive role in advancing knowledge and fostering professional excellence among ICAI Members and Students through its esteemed monthly publication, The Chartered Accountant. Some important initiatives in this regard are as under:

(II) Qualitative coverage in The Chartered Accountant Journal:

Blend of themes covered During the period, 220 articles on various themes and professional topics were included in the ICAI Journal. In addition, the journal also included other regular features that covered updates about the ICAI and its activities in coordination with various ICAI Committees.

July 2024 Issue brought Out as Special Edition The July 2023 issue of the ICAI Journal was released as a Collector's Edition to commemorate 75 Years of ICAI's Excellence, aligned with the Inspiring India@100 vision. Celebrating ICAI's legacy, the special edition featured the Institute's history, contributions to nation-building, and included notable articles by the President of IFAC and Chair of IESBA. The issue also carried inspirational CA Day messages from 32 eminent dignitaries, including the President of India, several Governors, Union Ministers, Chief Ministers, and other key officials. A special segment titled "Journey of Trust" highlighted ICAI's international presence, historical milestones such as the struggle for the 'Chartered' designation, ICAI stamps and logos, and iconic quotes, including a speech by India's first President.

The cover page symbolized ICAI's pivotal role over 75 years in shaping the profession and contributing to the vision of Building New India.

(III) Many Aspects of Digital Versions of The Chartered Accountant- Journal Upgraded for the convenience of Members and Students: • Journal in PDF format: As a valued added alternative to readers, particularly for separate content-wise downloads, the journal continues to be hosted in the PDF format. The archives of digital journal are available on ICAI website from July, 2002 onwards. • Journal on Digital Learning Hub: The electronic version of Journal, is available online on ICAI website www.icai.org as user-friendly e-magazine, is included in the Digital Learning Hub. This helped in providing knowledge through ICAI Learning Management System, besides supporting the Sustainability Drive of the ICAI. • Journal Highlight Emailers: The highlights of every issue of journal in capsule form and the President Message in the journal are mass-emailed to all the members. • Journal on Mobile: The e-journal is also available on mobile, compatible on iOS (IPad/IPhone etc.) and Android devices. The same can be accessed at http://www.icai.org/ under 'e-journal' tab. The e-journal is also available on ICAI Mobile App.

8.6 Infrastructure Development Committee (IDC) In the year 2014, Infrastructure Development Committee was formed as a non-standing Committee of the Institute.

Since 2014, the ICAI has a robust infrastructure policy in place, which ensures financial prudence and discipline.

This year, Committee has modified the Infrastructure Policy for Branches and Regional Councils/Offices. The policy defines what all facilities can be provided, composition of local Infrastructure Committees, policy and procedure for acquisition of land/ building, indicative area, permissible grant from Head Office, powers and delegation vested with various authorities within the Institute. Since the policy itself defines the financial powers, all infrastructure projects from the year 2014 onwards are being approved by IDC, instead of the Finance Committee. Since formulation of Infrastructure policy, the ICAI has initiated following projects:

Purchase of new Infrastructure Construction proposals approved Kannur, Jalandhar, Jabalpur, Goa, Gurugram, Moradabad, Pali, Agra, Gorakhpur, Karnal, Kishangarh, Latur, Patiala, Ujjan, Ratlam, Chengalpattu, Ahmedabad, Kota, Ernakulam, Himachal Pradesh, Jamnagar, Tirupati, Raipur, Bengaluru Gifted Property, ICOE Chennai (1.19 Acre), COE Guwahati, Kalyan Dombivli Ajmer, Surat, Bhopal, Rajamahendravaram, Centre of Excellence Jaipur, Bathinda, Bareilly, Jodhpur, Raipur, Kannur, Ghaziabad, Goa, Moradabad, Guntur, Agra, Gurugram, Rohini, Ratlam, Patiala, Kishangarh, Ujjain, Pali, COE Kolkata, Bengaluru Gifted Property, Ahmedabad, Chengalpattu, Ernakulam, Rohtak, Siliguri, Patna, Jabalpur, Jalandhar, ICOE Chennai Camp Office, Nagpur, Tirupati, Himachal Pradesh, Hubli (Audi), COE Kolkata, Ujjain, Latur, Patiala, Ratlam, Kota, Renovation of HO Building at C-1 Sector-1 Noida.

Out of total 179 Branches set up by the ICAI so far, 104 branches are having their own premises. 12 Branches (presently functioning from Rented Premises) who have procured land on which construction is either yet to be commenced or construction is under-way. 11 Branches (functioning from own premises) have procured land where either construction yet to be started or construction is under-way. 61 Branches do not own either land or building.
