8.7 International Affairs Committee (IAC)

(I) Initiatives of IAC for recognition of professional opportunities abroad The Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (ICAI) has established Mutual Recognition Agreements (MRAs) with 8 international professional bodies to facilitate mutual recognition of accountancy qualifications. These include CPA Australia, CAANZ (under renewal), SAICA(under renewal), CPA Canada(under renewal), MICPA, CPA Ireland, ICAEW, and ICAN. Agreements with CAANZ, SAICA, and CPA Canada are currently under renewal • Renewal of MRA with CPA Ireland In view of the amalgamation of CPA Ireland with the Chartered Accountants Ireland on September 01, 2024, after which the identity of CPA Ireland shall cease to exist but the new institute assures respect for existing reciprocity agreements, ICAI and CPA Ireland have progressed with their arrangements for the benefit of the members at either end. • Signing of Addendum with CA ANZ ICAI and CA ANZ previously had a reciprocity membership agreement. During the year, both the organizations actively engaged for review of course curriculum of each other and pending final review extended arrangement through an addendum for the benefit of the members. • Benchmarking of ICAI qualification by Ecctis To enhance global opportunities for its members, ICAI engaged Ecctis in 2019 to benchmark its qualifications against UK and UAE education standards. The study found ICAI's Intermediate level equivalent to a Bachelor's degree and the Final level equivalent to a Master's degree. Due to recent curriculum updates, ICAI has initiated a fresh evaluation with Ecctis to reaffirm its international standing, benefiting members seeking professional opportunities abroad. • Awareness sessions with MRA Partner ICAI has been actively promoting Mutual Recognition Agreements (MRAs) with global partners to create overseas opportunities for its members. During the reported period, ICAI organized 15 awareness sessions with CPA Australia across major cities and regions in India through both physical and virtual formats. Additionally, 6 awareness sessions were conducted with ICAEW in key cities nationwide, also via hybrid modes. ICAI also held a virtual awareness session with CPA Ontario, reaching a broad network of members and stakeholders to enhance understanding and engagement with international MRA opportunities.

(II) Globalizing ICAI's Brand Equity World Forum of Accountants- A resounding success ICAI organized the inaugural World Forum of Accountants (WOFA) from January 31 to February 2, 2025, at Yashobhoomi, New Delhi, aiming to position India as a global hub for finance and accounting. The landmark event gathered over 6,500 delegates from 32 countries and prominent global accounting bodies such as ICAEW, CPA Australia, CPA Canada, and SAFA, with an additional 800 participants attending virtually. Centered around the theme "Accountability Meets Innovation (AI): For a Sustainable Planet," the forum explored the integration of financial integrity, technology, and sustainability. WOFA featured 10 special sessions, 22 concurrent sessions, and 14 panel discussions addressed by over 200 international and national thought leaders. The event included 67 sponsors & exhibitors, 27 Indian firms, and an ICAI pavilion showcasing various committee initiatives.

Additionally, SAFA Committee and Board Strategy Meetings, and bilateral meetings with CPA Australia, CPA Canada, and CA Maldives were held on the sidelines, reinforcing ICAI's global engagement and leadership in the profession.

Partner in Nation Building – New Initiatives • ICAI established a dedicated Section 8 company, the ICAI International ADR Centre, to efficiently and effectively resolve commercial disputes. The International Alternative Dispute Resolution (ADR) Centre will offer arbitration, mediation, and conciliation services. • ICAI launched the beta version of Phase 1 of the External Confirmation Portal at the World Forum of Accountants. • ICAI rolled out a Certificate Course on Fund Management at IFSC, developed to address the growing professional opportunities within the IFSC in addition to introducing a Certificate Course on Compliance for Capital Market Intermediaries, designed to enhance members' understanding of regulatory frameworks and compliance practices in the capital markets.

ICAI Awards Ceremony Total 62 Awards under 5 categories were presented at the occasion. The awards were conferred by Shri Arjun Ram Meghwal, Union Minister of State (Independent Charge) for Law and Justice and Minister of State for Parliamentary Affairs. 75th Annual Function of ICAI Coinciding with the World Forum of Accountants, ICAI organized its 75th Annual Function on February 2, 2025.

Hon'ble Vice-President of India, Shri Jagdeep Dhankhar presided as Chief Guest at 75th Annual function of ICAI at World Forum of Accountants. The Annual Function featured awards presented to CA Rank Holders, Regional Councils, Branches, and Overseas Chapters.

ICAI- IFSCA Engagements The ICAI actively promotes India's GIFT City IFSC and enhances its global presence through high-level international delegations. These visits foster collaboration, raise the profile of Indian Chartered Accountants, and advance the accounting profession worldwide. • Australia (August 2024): The then President and Vice President joined an IFSCA delegation to Australia.

They met with ASIC to discuss collaboration, launched the revamped International Affairs Committee website, and promoted GIFT City's advantages and ICAI's role in the FinTech ecosystem. The delegation engaged with FinTech companies at a "Start-up Hub and FinTech ecosystem" meeting on 6th August, 2024, supported the industry, and attended a roundtable with Australian Self-Managed Super Funds and Asset Managers. The President addressed seminars on IFSC opportunities for UHNIs/HNIs and participated in a roundtable with accounting professionals, while the Vice President took part in a panel discussion. • USA (October 2024): The then President led an IFSCA delegation to New York and San Francisco to discuss GIFT IFSC opportunities with US financial services and universities. ICAI Chapters helped raise awareness, and the Florida Chapter was inaugurated, becoming ICAI's 51st overseas chapter. • UK (March 2025): The President ICAI, visited the UK with an IFSCA delegation to promote GIFT IFSC and gather insights into the UK's accounting profession. He addressed conferences on GIFT City business opportunities and addressed a "Roundtable Interaction on Book-keeping, Accounting, Taxation and Financial Crime Compliance Services in GIFT IFSC, India organized by ICAEW and the ICAI-UK (London) Chapter, highlighting outsourcing opportunities for UK firms. He also met with Deputy High Commissioner of India to the UK, and Minister (Economic) at the High Commission of India, to discuss opportunities for Indian Chartered Accountants to provide value-added services within the UK.

(III) Enhancing ICAI's Global Footprints • Enhancing Brand CA Globally through ICAI Overseas Chapters & Representative Offices ICAI has always been working untiringly in the direction of effectively and efficiently serving its members based in overseas. ICAI has now 54 Chapters and 31 Representative offices in 85 global cities in 47 Countries aiding to positioning the 'CA India' as a 'Brand' worldwide for generating more professional avenues for Indian Chartered Accountants. During the period under report, ICAI has inaugurated 4 Chapters in Florida, Zurich, Los Angeles and Atlanta. These Chapters are our brand Ambassadors and they uphold the mission of Indian Chartered Accountancy high in foreign soil. Representative office in Rotterdam in Netherlands and Kansas, Louisiana, Minnesota, Tennessee, and San Diego in USA were also launched. Representative offices abroad are opened where currently ICAI Chapters cannot be formed thus bringing together ICAI members abroad and enabling effective reach and service to its members, thus aiding to positioning the Brand "Indian CA" worldwide for generating more professional avenues for Indian Chartered Accountants. • Mobile App "Diaspora" for ICAI Overseas Members On October 25, 2024, ICAI launched the Android and iOS versions of its "Diaspora" mobile app to better connect with its international community of around 14,000 overseas members. The launch occurred during an event of the Toronto Chapter, coinciding with then-President CA Ranjeet Kumar Agarwal's visit to New York, San Francisco, and Toronto. The app provides members with easy access to information, enhances engagement, and fosters networking. It also synchronizes with ICAI's central database to identify members who have not updated their overseas professional addresses, improving the accuracy of membership records. • New Website of International Affairs Committee ICAI launched a separate website of the International Affairs Committee to enhance communication, foster collaboration, and provide a central hub for information and resources on August 5, 2024 coinciding with the visit of the then President CA. Ranjeet Kumar Agarwal to Australia. Recognizing the increasing importance of digital platforms in international affairs, the website aims to bridge geographical gaps, facilitate knowledge sharing among members and stakeholders, and promote transparency and accessibility to committee activities and initiatives. • Directions of the Council for Accreditation and Functioning of Chapters Outside India ICAI developed a comprehensive guidelines "Directions of the Council for Accreditation and Functioning of Chapters Outside India" for strengthening ICAI overseas Chapters in order to serve the members abroad in an effective and efficient way. • ICAI Best Oversesa Chapter Awards 2024 Since 2013, the International Affairs Committee (IAC) has annually presented the ICAI Best Chapter Awards to recognize overseas Chapters for their outstanding efforts in promoting the 'Indian CA' brand and fostering member networking abroad. The awards honor Chapters' exemplary achievements in advancing the Chartered Accountancy profession based on defined criteria. In 2025, the awards were presented to 12 overseas Chapters during ICAI's 75th Annual Day function held on February 2, coinciding with the World Forum of Accountants.

The event was presided over by Hon'ble Vice-President of India, as the Chief Guest. • ICAI's Representatives on International Bodies From time to time, ICAI representatives have been at the leadership positions of various International Accounting bodies. This ensures the ICAI remains at the forefront of emerging trends and knowledge development. Currently, ICAI is being represented in the following Board/Committee of :➢ International Federation of Accountants (IFAC) – Board Member; Advisory Group Members;

Independent Standard Setting Board Member and Technical Advisors ➢ Confederation of Asian and Pacific Accountants (CAPA) –Board Member & President ➢ South Asian Federation of Accountants (SAFA) – Advisor, Board & Assembly Member, Technical Advisors, Committee Chair & Committee Members ➢ Chartered Accountants Worldwide (CAW) – Board Member, Technical Advisor. ➢ Edinburgh Group –Chairman. ➢ Asean Federation of Accountants (AFA) – Associate Member & Working Committee Members ➢ IFRS Foundation- Member, IFRS SME Implementation Group ➢ Pan African Federation of Accountants (PAFA) – Affiliate Member ➢ XBRL International- Board Member

(IV) Delegations visiting ICAI • Visit of delegation from CAIR CEO & Director, Centre for Australia India Relations (CAIR) visited ICAI, New Delhi and met with the then President, ICAI on September 19, 2024 wherein deliberations were held regarding various opportunities for ICAI members in Australia. • Training sessions organized by ICAI for the delegation from ICPA Kenya A 12-member delegation from the Institute of Certified Public Accountants of Kenya (ICPAK), led by ViceChairman CPA Prof. Elizabeth Nthambi Muvui, visited ICAI during the World Forum of Accountants in February 2025. ICAI organized a two-day training for them, where the then President and other senior ICAI officials welcomed the delegates and briefed them on key initiatives like UDIN. The visit also focused on renewing the 2019 MoU between ICAI and ICPAK to strengthen mutual cooperation in professional development. Training sessions covered UDIN, AI, digital learning, audit quality, research, MSME initiatives, and support for members in practice. The Kenyan delegation appreciated the valuable insights gained from the comprehensive training.

8.8 Management Committee Management Committee, constituted in 2015 as non-standing Committee of the Council, is mandated to consider matters pertaining to formation of Branches, setting up of Chapters abroad, MoUs/ MRAs with national/ international bodies, appointment of central auditors of ICAI, annual accounts of the Institute, matters referred by the Central Government and other regulatory bodies, proposals for amendments in the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, Rules and Regulations framed thereunder, Regional Councils and Branches matters, Members/CA related matters and proposals received from other committees/departments of the Institute having administrative and policy implications and making its recommendations to the Council wherever required.

8.9 Public Relations Committee (PRC) The Public Relations Committee has been working towards strengthen and enhance the brand image of the ICAI as a premier accountancy body and the sole regulatory authority for the profession of Chartered Accountancy in India through various ways and means, as considered appropriate within the framework of the CA Act. The PR Committee further endeavors to foster good relationship with Media and other Stakeholders in coordination with various committees of ICAI to provide smooth communication, information and to enhance the visibility of ICAI.

Media serves as one of the most strategic tools for effectively conveying opinions and perspectives to the public, policymakers, and key stakeholders. In recent times, ICAI has proactively leveraged the media to communicate its views and decisions with clarity and impact. The Public Relations Committee has not only expanded ICAI's media footprint in terms of reach and volume but has also built meaningful long-term relationships with key national media.

(I) Enhancing presence of ICAI in Media • Periodical Media Interactions with prominent Publications to share ICAI's views on important matters, initiatives, achievements and key developments. • Exclusive Articles i.e One-in-three CAs in India is a woman, 7 essential tips every aspiring CA must know for exam preparation, New non-financial accounting norms in the works, as ESG grabs centre stage etcwere published • Other Important News Coverage- ICAI's major updates, including the Supreme Court tax audit limit ruling, disciplinary actions against EY and PwC, Pre-Budget Memorandum proposals, Delhi High Court exam decision, and views on SA 600 and SA 299 revisions, received wide media coverage. • Press Releases on Important Announcements / Events- ICAI issued 49 press releases and held 8 press conferences during the year, along with over 35 one-on-one media interactions to enhance communication.

As a result, more than 1,100 news clippings appeared in top newspapers and news agencies, and over 1,800 reports were published online, significantly boosting ICAI's media presence and outreach.

(II) Promotional activities • 10th International Day of Yoga- Since 2015, ICAI has celebrated International Day of Yoga through its Regional Councils and Branches. On June 21, 2024, the 10th IDY was marked with programs across 5 Regional Councils and 176 Branches nationwide. Uniform branding materials, grants, and guidelines were provided, encouraging enthusiastic participation from members, students, and their families. • CA Run for Viksit Bharat- To celebrate ICAI's 75th Chartered Accountants Day and support PM Modi's vision of Viksit Bharat, a nationwide "CA Run for Viksit Bharat" was held on June 30, 2024. Members and students globally participated, with events across all 5 Regional Councils, 176 Branches, and foreign chapters starting simultaneously at 6 AM. The New Delhi run began at ICAI HQ and ended at Major Dhyan Chand National Stadium, with Hon'ble Minister of State of Corporate Affairs & Road Transport and Highways as Chief Guest. Uniform branding materials and grants were provided, and the event was widely promoted through print, TV, radio, and social media. • Chartered Accountant's Day-2024- As ICAI celebrated its 75 years of trust, on July 1, 2024, on this occasion a grand celebration was organised at Bharat Mandapam in New Delhi. The event was graced by Hon'ble Minister of Road Transport and Highways as the Chief Guest along with Secretary, Ministry of Corporate Affairs. Also present were the then President, ICAI; the then Vice-President ICAI, galaxy of Past Presidents, Central and Regional Council Members of ICAI and Secretary, ICAI.

The event was widely publicized through following activities: ➢ Print Media Campaign: Towards publicizing CA Day, Print Advt. were developed and published in prominent mainline and financial publications. Post event publicity was also done by publishing an event synopsis report in the media. ➢ TV Campaign: The 75-year CA Day celebrations of ICAI were publicized on various Business/News Channels through various elements and news reports. The event was also captured and aired on Zee Business. ➢ Radio Campaign – Radio spots publicizing the event were played on prominent FM stations pan India. ➢ Social Media Campaign – A Social Media campaign was run across ICAI social media platforms through Creatives, short videos, video messages. Post event videos capturing the Grand Celebrations were also developed and posted on ICAI social media platforms with garnered lakhs of impressions. ➢ OOH Campaign – Hoardings were designed and put up for outdoor branding covering important locations around ITO to venue - Bharat Mandapam publicizing CA Day. ➢ Standee- Major achievements and initiatives of last 75 years were compiled and standees were developed and displayed during the CA Day Function held at New Delhi ➢ Audio Visual- - An Audio Visual for CA Day was developed showcasing ICAI pivotal role as partner in nation building which captured the bytes of various dignitaries who have graced ICAI events in the past.

The messages of President & Vice-President were also recorded and shared with RCs & Branches and uploaded on social media. ➢ Goodwill Messages: On occasion of CA Day a special issue of ICAI Journal i.e. July, 2024 was brought by the editorial board, this issue captured wishes of Policical leaders and eminent dignitaries.

PRC arranged and followed up with the offices of the dignitaries for these messages. Over 35 messages from dignitaries were received.

(III) Budget -2024 Activities • Budget Reactions, Economic Survey and Pre and Post Budget Memorandum was shared with the media and was covered prominently in more than 45 publications (print & online) and in various TV Channels like CNBC TV 18, CNBC Awaaz, Zee Business, Zee TV and Aajtak. • A live session of the President, ICAI was aired on Zee News immediately after the Budget Speech.

(IV) Nationwide Plantation Drive on Independence Day On 15th August, marking the 75th year celebrations of the Institute, a nationwide plantation drive was organised through RCs & Branches. Around 4000 saplings were planted on this special day. This initiative has been undertaken jointly by SRSB, PRC and Group for Celebration of the 75th year of ICAI.

(V) Felicitation of Sr. Members-aged 75 years or above To mark ICAI's 75th anniversary, senior members aged 75 or above (as of July 1, 2024) were felicitated to honor their contributions to the CA profession. A special Felicitation Kit with a plaque, certificate, lapel pin, and car stickers was prepared. Regional Councils and Branches were asked to honor these members during events or visits.

By May 2025, over 2,000 senior members had been felicitated nationwide.

(VI) Each One Teach One ICAI, committed to nurturing the next generation, launched the 'Each One Teach One' mentorship initiative on July 1, 2024, following call of Union Minister, Commerce and Industry to encourage members to mentor students, promoting peer learning and knowledge sharing. A dedicated website (https://eoto.icai.org/) was also launched for registration and participation.

(VII) Publicity & Promotion of World Forum of Accountants (WOFA) ICAI hosted the World Forum of Accountants 2025, its largest accounting event, at Yashobhoomi, New Delhi, themed "Accountability Meets Innovation (AI): For a Sustainable Planet." The three-day convention saw over 6,000 delegates from 40+ countries, 40+ sessions, and 200+ speakers. A special WOFA Anthem was launched to capture the forum's spirit, widely publicized and well-received. A media briefing was also held to highlight the event's global scale, speakers, and key themes.Towards branding & publicity of this Mega event, the PRC has undertaken various activities i.e Print Advertisement in Economic Times, Electronic Media- TVC Promos, Postevent Episode, Radio Jingles aired, Digital/OOH Campaigns, Audio Visuals, Branding at the venue and a Visual Memoir in form of Coffee Table Book (CTB) of WOFA 2025.

(IX) Other activities: • Press Release on each day capturing the full day of the event were issued • Quote of Dignitaries gracing the event were also immediately shared with the Media • Live tweets and photographs of each session were posted on WOFA Social Media • Standees & Banners for RCs & Branches were developed and shared • Advt. in CA Journal promoting WOFA was published in Nov. issue • WOFA teaser videos was developed & circulated to all Members through Whatsapp • Various Social Media Creatives developed and uploaded on ICAI & WOFA Social Media handles on daily basis • WOFA Photo Template was shared with delegates for branding

(X) ICAI Awards 2024 ICAI hosted the first-ever ICAI Awards 2024 on February 1, 2025 at WOFA, New Delhi, consolidating awards from five committees into a single platform. 62 awards were presented across categories including financial reporting, sustainability, local bodies, industry, and women excellence. Hon'ble MoS, Ministry of Law and Justice presented the awards. A special Garuda-inspired memento was given to winners, and the event was widely publicized.

(XI) Publicity of 75th ICAI Annual Function A half-page color advt. publicizing the event was published in major publications- TOI & The Hindu Business Line. The Annual Function was graced by Hon'ble Vice-President of India as Chief Guest, Member of Parliament along with Secretary General, Rajya Sabha. Press Releases was issued post the event.

(XII) Election of New Torchbearers – 2025-26 The election of the new torchbearers (2025-26) was publicized widely amongst members, stakeholders and public at large through Press Release and First Press Conference of the year, Publicity on Social Media, Mass SMS and Collaterals designs.

(XIII) Press Meet & Interview On March 6, 2025, ICAI held a press meet with key media houses to launch the annual theme 'VISHWASNIYA' and share the year's action plan and priorities. The event received wide coverage across print, online, and TV platforms. ICAI also provided inputs to major publications, resulting in articles on topics like the new Income-Tax Bill, campus placement trends, and forex accounting norms.

(XIV) Publicity & Promotion of various events ICAI Startup Sphere 2024 held in Bangaluru, Special discussion on the Union Budget 2024-2025, 2nd Edition of RESOLVE-2024, ICAI MSME & StartUp Yatra, AI Innovation Summit – AIS 2024 at Hyderabad on July 16, 2024, ICAI Convocation Ceremony, First Audit Day, Local Governance Synergy Conclave, Global Ethics Day, Opening of COE Kolkata, Accounting Standards Day through Print media, electronice media, Radio campaign, finance media, business media etc.

(XV) Press Conferences • Year Ender Press Conference in New Delhi- On December 23, 2024, ICAI held a press conference in New Delhi, attended by major media outlets. The President and Vice-President highlighted ICAI's key achievements and initiatives, resulting in coverage by over 90 print, online, and TV publications. • Press Conferences organized across the country- PRC organized press conferences at various events and locations—including Jaipur, New Delhi, Hyderabad, Bengaluru, Kolkata, and multiple branches—where the President shared updates on key initiatives. These were widely covered in media and online. • ICAI Stands with Nation -During Operation 'SINDOOR', ICAI urged its 14.5 lakh members and students to support the nation through a webcast on May 9, 2025, which received over 1.5 million social media impressions. Promotional materials were designed, mass communications sent, live tweets shared and a press release issued, resulting in coverage across 100+ media outlets. • ICAI Participation in the Powering Bharat Summit 2025- ICAI participated in the Powering Bharat Summit 2025 on May 16 at Taj Palace, New Delhi, alongside key ministers and leaders. The ICAI President spoke on India's vision for economic leadership and Viksit Bharat@2047. His address was broadcast on CNBC TV18. • Publicity & Promotion of All India Managing Committee Members Meet (AIMCMM 2025) March 21st - 23rd at New Delhi through Print Media and distribution of Lapel Pins to Management Committee Members of RCs & Branches. • Audio Visuals Developed during the year i.e Teaser of the event 'World Forum of Accountants (WOFA), 5 Committee specific Avs, Election AVs-2, ICAI Awards • Audio Visual showcasing ICAI Vision of the Year – VISHWASNIYA- To promote the year's vision 'VISHWASNIYA,' the PR Committee produced an audio-visual highlighting key focus areas like integrity, sustainability, digital transformation, and youth development etc. The video, launched at AIMCMM 2025, will guide ICAI's initiatives across all levels. • Monthly Videos highlighting significant achievements of ICAI- Every month, video bytes of President, ICAI highlighting the monthly significant achievements of ICAI are recorded and accordingly a short video was developed by PRC. The same was then uploaded on social media accounts of ICAI and sent to all members of the Institute via Whatsapp. • Association with Doordarshan- To expand its reach beyond print, ICAI partnered with Prasar Bharati for one year to air promos and L Bands on Doordarshan's 19 national and regional channels. This collaboration promoted ICAI initiatives during Budget Day and Independence Day, the Financial & Tax Literacy Campaign, and the World Forum of Accountants (WOFA). Promotion of the Certificate Course for Accountants of Panchayats and Municipal Bodies is in the pipeline. • Letter to Editor in response to misreporting by media- ICAI took strong action against negative media reports that tarnished the image of the Chartered Accountancy profession. Letters were sent to editors of prominent newspapers requesting corrections to restore the profession's reputation and ensure accurate public representation.

Publications • Year Book: 2024-25 • Treasure Trove of Wisdom from Visionaries-Speeches of Past Presidents, ICAI-6th Edition • Coffee Table Book of WOFA

8.10 Research Committee The Research Committee of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India, established in 1955, is one of the oldest technical committees dedicated to fostering a strong research culture to enhance professional services. Its primary objective is to conduct research and strengthen institutional capacity in accounting and allied fields for the benefit of the profession and the nation.The Committee actively promotes research and innovation through various projects, programs, workshops, and seminars. It also focuses on developing strategies for global collaboration in key areas of international importance. The Committee conducts research on diverse areas such as the profession, economy, education, business, industry, health, and infrastructure. It provides research-based insights to support government policy, offers recommendations, and prepares representations on accounting and professional matters.

Focused on promoting high-quality research, it leads strategic initiatives to improve financial reporting and offers thought leadership on emerging trends and best practices affecting the profession.

(I) Research Project in Progress • Corporate Reporting Practices of Listed and Unlisted Companies in India • Accounting for Digital Assets • An Exploratory Analysis of MUDRA and PMMY in Fostering Growth of Micro Enterprises • The Rise of Green Investment: Investors' Intention and Mediating Role of Theory of Planned Behavior on Sustainable Investment Propensity • Tax-to-GDP Ratio and Sustainable Growth: An Empirical Analysis of India's Economic Development • "Revitalizing India's Legal Landscape: Analysing Obsolete Economic and Commercial Laws for Viksit Bharat@2047" • REIT's and Its Emerging Scope in India • The AI Audit Revolution: Exploring Opportunities and Challenges in Audit & Accounting Practice • Decoding the Development Dilemma of Dhubri District and Roadmap for its Inclusive Growth • Study Of Current Socio Economic Affairs In Sehore District Of Madhya Pradesh And The Way Forward • Unveiling Varanasi- Exploring the dynamics of India's Spiritual Capital • Mahua Flowers: Regulatory, Economic, and Social Opportunities

(II) Promoting Excellence in Financial Reporting Practices ICAI Awards for Excellence in Financial Reporting 2023-24- Since 1958, ICAI has organized the 'Awards for Excellence in Financial Reporting' to recognize entities adhering to reporting standards. The 2023-24 awardees were selected through a rigorous four-tier process, with the jury meeting held on January 14, 2025, chaired by Tourism Finance Corporation of India Ltd. The awards ceremony took place on February 1, 2025, at Yashobhoomi, New Delhi, where 19 awards—6 Gold Shields and 13 Silver Shields—were presented.

(III) Recognition of Research Scholars ICAI International Research Award 2024- Since 2020, the ICAI Research Committee has been organizing the

"ICAI International Research Awards" to promote innovation in Accounting, Auditing, Economics, Finance, and Taxation. The 2024 virtual jury meeting was held on August 8, chaired by President of CPA Australia, with esteemed international members. The awards ceremony took place on August 30, 2024, in Chandigarh, where 11 awards were presented: 4 Gold, 4 Silver, and 3 Bronze Shields.

(IV) Schemes for promoting Research Culture ICAI Doctoral Scholarship Scheme- ICAI Doctoral Scholarship Scheme seeks to provide requisite support by way of monthly scholarship to the eligible candidates with outstanding academic credentials, intellectual curiosity and needed discipline to make scholarly contribution. This scheme is open for ICAI members who are not more than 40 years of age, who should be Full Time Student at the University/College/Institution while pursuing Ph.D. and not availing any such scholarship from any other Institution. The scholarship of Rs. 75,000 per month for 3 years will be given for maximum 5 scholars annually.

(V) ICAI Research Projects Scheme- The launch of the ICAI Research Project Scheme in 2020 demonstrates the Research Committee's commitment to empowering and supporting researchers interested in conducting insightful investigations on contemporary topics, ultimately enhancing advancements in the field of accounting and its related domains. ICAI Research Project Scheme is a scheme which aims to fund research projects to be undertaken by members having experience of more than 10 years either in practice or in employment or experienced research scholars of a recognised University having published research papers in any referred Journal. The maximum amount of Rs. 10 lacs will be reimbursed as research expenditure. The Researchers are required to complete the research project within 6 months after the research proposal is approved by the Research Committee. The Scheme is open round the year.summarize.

Since its introduction, various projects have been awarded and completed under the Scheme as follows – • Internal Control System in State Owned Universities A Study to Formulate Internal Control Manual • Impact of Digital Transformation Strategies on Financial Performance in the Indian Manufacturing Sector • How Indian Companies can play a pivotal role in the supply chain to Australia? • Inching towards Tax Certainty: Neoteric Domestic Dispute Mechanism for Cross-Border Taxation • Analysis and evaluation of Indian Startups in non-metropolitan areas and selected metropolitan areas-An Untold Story • Money laundering and scams "THROUGH" Multi-State Urban Cooperative Credit Societies in India • Taxation aspects of circulars issued by Regulatory Authorities- A study of IRDA Circulars • Evaluating the effect of scientific meditation on employee burnout: A multinational study • The emerging role of auditors and CFOs in addressing Risk Management: A New perspective • Finance & Tax Literacy for Start-Ups • Taxation on Non-Resident Artistes or Sportspersons – Domestically and Internationally Also, a few projects are in pipeline.

(VI) ICAI Short Term Research Studies Scheme 2024 Research Committee has introduced the Short Term Research Studies Scheme in 2023 to spearheading the innovative and short-term research projects that may lead to breakthroughs in the critical issues. This scheme provides a platform for sharing new ideas, innovative research findings and thought-provoking insights on various contemporary topics. The weightage would be given to applicant having experience of at least 5 years in the Research domain and a PhD Holder. The Permissible Honorarium of Rs. 1,00,000 would be provided. The applicants will be given the time duration of three months for completing the research projects. The scheme is open round the year.

Six projects have been approved under the scheme on the following topics which are in progress – • REIT'S and its Emerging Scope in India • The AI Audit Revolution: Exploring Opportunities and Challenges in Audit & Accounting Practice • Study Of Current Socio-Economic Affairs in Sehore District Of Madhya Pradesh and the Way Forward • Decoding the Development Dilemma of Dhubri District and Roadmap for its Inclusive Growth • Unveiling Varanasi- Exploring the dynamics of India's Spiritual Capital • Mahua Flowers: Regulatory, Economic, and Social Opportunities

(VII) Capacity Building Initiatives • The Committee organized the "Academia Meet with Universities/Institutions" on May 17, 2024, at New Delhi, to promote research and innovation through interaction and knowledge exchange among leading academicians. The event, attended by distinguished university and business school researchers, aimed to advance research for the benefit of the profession and economy • An interactive discussion on research advancements was held on May 29, 2024 at New Delhi, bringing together Ph.D. holders and research-oriented Chartered Accountants nationwide. The event focused on fostering collaboration and exploring new research areas to benefit the profession and the economy. • The Committee organized an "Interactive Discussion on Excellence in Financial Reporting" on June 25, 2024, at New Delhi. The event focused on preparing for accurate reporting, avoiding common errors, and updating on emerging trends in financial and non-financial reporting. Emphasis was placed on the importance of high-quality reporting in building organizational value, trust, and market confidence. The session highlighted that rigorous disclosure is both a regulatory requirement and an ethical business practice. • The Committee organizes live panel discussions on contemporary research topics to foster continuous learning, innovation, and collaboration among researchers, institutions, and industries. Key themes include emerging trends in financial reporting, best practices in annual reports, risk-based controls, tax implications, challenges in MSME financial reporting, integrated reporting, and the role of technology in reporting processes.

8.11 Strategy, Perspective Planning and Monitoring Committee (SPP&MC) The Strategy Perspective Planning and Monitoring Committee is a non-standing committee of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (ICAI) serves a critical role in shaping long-term ICAI Vision and objectives. The primary purpose of this committee is to engage in strategic planning, ensuring that the ICAI develops and implements effective strategies to meet its goal. The committee is committed to effective risk management, identifying potential challenges, and adapting strategies to navigate a dynamic business environment. By analysing various perspective, trends and factors, the committee aim to provide a comprehensive outlook for the organization's future. Additionally, it plays a critical role in monitoring the execution of strategic initiatives, assessing their impact, and making adjustments as necessary.

Activities/Initiatives:

(I) Preparation of ICAI Vision 2049-Collaboration with IIMA In pursuit of a forward-looking strategy for the Institute, the ICAI initiated the development of ICAI Vision 2049 through a collaborative engagement with the Indian Institute of Management Ahmedabad (IIMA). This collaboration aims to outline a comprehensive roadmap that aligns the Institute's long-term goals with emerging global, technological, and regulatory trends.

(II) Formation of Group for Preparation of the Vision Document Pursuant to the decision of the competent authority, a dedicated Group was constituted under the aegis of the Strategy, Perspective Planning and Monitoring Committee (SPPMC) for the preparation of the Vision document.

(III) Submission of Revised Draft Inputs were sought by the IIMA via conducting series of interaction with President, Vice President, Central Council Members, Secretary, Faculty, Boards of Studies, senior officers and members of ICAI for preparation of ICAI Vision 2049. Draft questionnaires were circulated to seek views of members, students, present and past Council Members, and employees. A meeting of past Presidents was held on 21st March, 2024 at Jaipur to seek their views on ICAI Vision 2049. The views expressed therein were collated and placed before the SPPMC at its 10th meeting held on 16th April 2024 at New Delhi. Further, a meeting with past Council members was held on 27th April, 2024 at New Delhi. The views expressed therein were also collated. With the approval of President, the summary of responses received on questionnaire, summarisation of views expressed by past Presidents, present Council members and past council members had been shared with IIMA on 3rd May 2024. IIMA thereafter submitted their first draft to SPPMC on for its consideration on 5th June 2024. The same was considered by the Group wherein all members of the Council were invited. The members therein made certain suggestions for improvement in the draft Vision Document which were communicated to IIMA on 12th June, 2024.

Subsequently, IIMA submitted the second draft report via email dated 25th June 2024. The revised draft was acknowledged by the office, and further discussions were held regarding the structure and content of the Vision 2049 Document. In addition, some further observations have been communicated to IIMA for inclusion in the final version. IIMA has agreed to incorporate these inputs and will submit the final report shortly, along with a concise 5–6 page Vision Document aligned with the structure and format of the ICAI Vision 2030. The final Vision 2049 document will serve as a strategic blueprint to guide ICAI's evolution over the next 25 years, reinforcing its role as a global leader in the accounting profession. Vision 2049 will mark a significant milestone in shaping ICAI's future trajectory with clarity, purpose, and strategic foresight.

8.12 Central Council Library The Central Council Library of the Institute caters to the information requirements of its stakeholders. Its aim is to provide comprehensive and up to date collection of primary and secondary print and non-print material to the present and anticipated members/students, research scholars and officials of ICAI. Library has assumed greater responsibilities of serving committees, departments in imparting knowledge and valuable information through books, e-books, journals, magazines, on-line databases, print newspapers as well as e-newspapers. Central council library is responsible for updating and providing journals and books required for the various committees work. The Central Council Library is fully computerized and working through Liberty- library management software. Library material including database of Books, Journals & Articles can be searched through Subject, Author, Title, Topic, Keyword, & Publisher wise. These records are available on Internet Online Services www.icai.org under "Central Council Library"-online search OPAC-Liberty for the books, Journals, articles etc. in the library.

Under the Column "Accountants Browser", an index of articles relevant to accounting profession are published every month in the journal "The Chartered Accountant". One may note that The "Accountants Browser" is an index of important/Professional Articles with archives of past articles. Reference service from library is also provided to the Researchers & Scholars, faculties, students and members. A number of online databases have been acquired by the Library, details of which are available on www.icai.org – Central Council Library. These On-line knowledge databases have been installed in the Central Council Library and can be accessed in house only, to facilitate the search for required material by the Members, Faculties and Research Scholars. Several e-journals have also been subscribed in the Library. 8.13 Right to Information Act, 2005 The basic objective of the Right to Information Act is to empower the citizens, to acclaim transparency and accountability in the working of every public authority. The Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (ICAI) a statutory body set up by an Act of Parliament i.e. The Chartered Accountants Act, 1949 is a public authority as envisaged under Section 2(h) of the RTI Act,2005. In compliance of the provisions of the RTI Act, 2005 and direction of the Central Information Commission, officers of the Institute have been designated as Central Public Information Officer, Central Assistant Public Information Officer, First Appellate Authority (FAA). A large number of RTIs is being received by RTI Cell, ICAI from the students, members, other stakeholders & citizens and the same have been responded.

Disclosure under Section 4(1) (b) of the Right to Information Act, 2005 Further, in terms of the Section 4(1)(b) of the Right to Information Act, 2005, necessary disclosures have been made by the Institute by hosting them on the website of the Institute www.icai.org and the same are updated from time to time. During the period 2024-25, it is to note that total 2,29,381 (two lakh twenty nine thousand and three hundred eighty-one) applications have been received which include the applications requesting for certified copies ( CA Foundation, Intermediate & Final ) of various examinations conducted by the ICAI besides Disciplinary functions and HRD & Administration. The Bifurcation of the aforesaid RTI applications is given below;

June to Dec 2024 Certified Copies of answer sheet Others Total Foundation Intermediate Final 21,164 79,150 49,287 1,50,177 Total number of RTI Application received Jan to May 2025 Certified Copies of answer sheet Others Total Foundation Intermediate Final 14175 43719 20966 79,204 Total number of RTI Application received Further, 26 number of hearings of the Central Information Commission have been attended and a good number of orders of First Appellate Authority have been responded.

9. DIRECTORATES

9.1 Centre for Audit Quality Directorate (CAQD) The ICAI established the Centre for Audit Quality Directorate ("CAQD") in 2020, primarily focusing on the qualitative aspects of the audit function and audit quality. It is a Special Purpose Directorate, ensuring consistent improvement in audit quality and boost in Indian firm's global standing. It also seeks to provide a conducive environment for research and to take up more research-oriented projects to set the criteria for enhancing and maintaining the Audit Quality. It supports research programs on Audit Quality by enhancing interactions among various stakeholders and regulators involved in law enforcement. The Centre for Audit Quality Directorate is aiming at continuous investment in the contemporary education and training for professional accountants and auditors through Special short term duration courses for participants to induct the requisite skill set on the contemporary areas. It strives to develop guidance and training programmes to assist audit teams to undertake effective root cause analysis for audit quality improvement.

ACTIVITIES UNDERTAKEN BY CENTRE FOR AUDIT QUALITY DIRECTORATE

(I) UTILITIES DEVELOPED BY CAQ The CAQD develops and provides technological resources to members to facilitate smooth conduct of quality audit and assurance. In line with this objective, the CAQD continued its efforts to support audit firms by developing and updating various tools and utilities aimed at enhancing audit quality, operational efficiency, and regulatory compliance.

During the year, the following utilities were developed/ updated: • Utility for self-evaluation under AQMM v 2.0- Release of a new Utility- This Utility will assist the firms to self -evaluate their firm's maturity level on the basis of Audit Quality Indicators defined under AQMM v

2.0. This will enable the firms to identify areas where competencies are strong or lacking and then develop a road map for achieving a higher level of audit maturity. • Utility for Bank Branch Audit- 2025 Edition- Updation of existing Utility- To help the members in smooth conduct of the bank branch audits, the Centre for Audit Quality Directorate (CAQD) has developed an excel utility based on the Guidance Note on Audit of Banks and the Technical Guide on Long Form Audit Report issued by AASB. The Utility for Bank Branch Audit- 2025 Edition has been updated, incorporating the changes in the Guidance Note on Audit of Banks (2025 Edition).

In the previous years, the Directorate had successfully introduced several key utilities, including: • Utility for Determining Materiality: The Utility assists auditors in assessing audit materiality thresholds by categorizing risk parameters and evaluating the impact of material misstatements. • Reviewing and Monitoring Tool on Standards on Auditing Compliance: The Tool facilitates compliance with Standards on Auditing by providing comprehensive checklists, enabling easy review, monitoring, and documentation. Progress is tracked through a 'Work Status' feature summarized in the Dashboard. • Utility on Engagement Letters: The Utility streamlines the generation of engagement letters for assurance clients, enabling auditors to efficiently create multiple letters using a customizable template, saving time and reducing complexity.

(II) PUBLICATIONS RELEASED BY CAQ • Audit Quality Maturity Model version 2.0 (AQMM v 2.0): • Implementation Guide for AQMM v 2.0 • Significant Audit Matters • Firm Manual Model Referencer • Analysis of Modified Opinions

(III) PROGRAMMES/ WEBINARS ORGANISED BY CAQ • Interactive Meeting for Audit Quality Maturity Model version 2.0 (AQMM v 2.0) at BKC MumbaiThe Centre for Audit Quality organized an interactive meeting on 23rd August 2024 at BKC, Mumbai to gather feedback on the Audit Quality Maturity Model version 2.0 (AQMM v 2.0) based on which the Centre for Audit Quality has developed the Implementation Guide for AQMM v 2.0. The meeting was chaired by Convenor, CAQ. • Training Programme organised for the officials of Supreme Audit Institution (SAI) of Syria- A training session was held on 12th September 2024, at ICAI Bhawan, New Delhi for 13 officers from the Supreme Audit Institution (SAI) of Syria, as part of a program under the Indian Technical and Economic Cooperation (ITEC) initiative by the Ministry of External Affairs. Organized at the request of iCISA (C&AG's training institute), the session focused on ICAI's Quality Control and Assurance practices, and was conducted by senior ICAI officials from various Boards and Directorates. The Syrian delegates expressed gratitude for the valuable insights and ICAI's warm hospitality. • Quality Café sessions - A monthly virtual series on audit quality- The CAQ organises Quality Café series in virtual mode with a 2-hour interactive panel discussion by Audit Experts wherein the elements that contribute to audit quality are discussed. The objective of the series is to enhance awareness and understanding of the important elements of audit quality. During the period, 2 Quality Café sessions were organized, which were attended by approximately 1046 participants. • Other Programmes The Centre for Audit Quality Directorate organized 15 Programs on Audit Quality Aspects.

(IV) OTHER INITIATIVES: 'X' (Twitter) handle of CAQ A dedicated 'X' (formerly Twitter) handle has been created by the CAQ to engage with members and support the enhancement of their awareness and understanding of various aspects of audit quality. As part of this initiative, a regular 'Did You Know?' series—featuring informative Q&A-style posts—is being shared to disseminate key insights on various elements of audit quality.

9.2 Centre of Excellence Directorate (CoED) The Centre of Excellence Directorate, established by ICAI, is dedicated to advancing research, thought leadership and learning with global standards. Currently, ICAI operates three Centres of Excellence located in Hyderabad, Jaipur and Kolkata. Over the next three years, ICAI envisions expanding this network by establishing eight additional CoEs across the country, focused on promoting research, knowledge development and capacity building.

During the year 2024–25, seven meetings of the CoE Directorate were conducted.

(I) Major Achievements/Milestones • Placed a human structure in the form of a Director cum Dean to head the Centre of Excellence (CoE) Hyderabad along with Research Personnel with a focus on strengthening ICAI's leadership position through enhanced collaboration, research, teaching, training, strategic planning, and advancing the Institute's vision. • Advanced research facilities, including software, databases and journals - SAS Software, STATA Software, Database: Prowess Dx & Prowess on web, Prime Databases: Insiders Database, Indian Boards Database & Key Managerial Personnel Database, have been introduced initially at CoE Hyderabad to support highquality academic and professional research in line with global standards.

(II) Research Projects at CoE Hyderabad • MSMEs in Jammu & Kashmir: Catalysts for Regional Development • Ten Years of the Women Director Mandate: Insights for Strengthening Gender-Inclusive Governance in India • How are MSMEs contributing to Nation Building in terms of GDP, Tax, Employment, Performance? • One Nation One Election (ONOE): Financial and Policy Implications

(III) Advanced Flagship Programs / Residential Training Programs A one of its kind five-day residential training program was conducted in January 2025 titled "Advanced Flagship Program: Empowering Chartered Accountants as Trusted Advisors – Unlocking the Potential of Family Enterprises," which saw participation from 27 members. The program was designed to equip members with deep domain expertise, strategic insights and practical skills to navigate complex professional challenges and serve as trusted advisors in evolving business environments.

(IV) Knowledge Series Live Webcasts with Thought Leaders Organized 2 live webcasts on topics "Jamsetji Tata's Blueprint for Corporate Success" and Economic and Fiscal Implications of the Proposed 'One Nation, One Election' Initiative. These Knowledge Series Webcasts are a flagship initiative designed to engage ICAI members with eminent thought leaders across diverse domains.

(V) Programs organised at various Centres of Excellence The Centre of Excellence Hyderabad and Jaipur combined, successfully organised 97 residential programs during the period engaging a total of 7,583 stakeholders, including 76 programs at CoE Hyderabad with 5,772 participants and 21 programs at CoE Jaipur with 1,811 participants.

9.3 DEVELOPMENT OF INTERNATIONAL TRADE, SERVICES AND WTO DIRECTORATE (DITS & WTO) (under International Affairs Committee) Development of International Trade, Services & WTO Directorate of ICAI aims at promoting bilateral investments in order to align with PM Modi's vision and spotlighting accounting and finance as crucial export services to be vital amidst global services trade and India's expanding FTAs. This emphasis offered Chartered Accountants diverse opportunities, evident in ICAI's global footprint, showcasing their international reach. Beyond branding, the Directorate aided trade navigation, fosters expertise, connects professionals, and influences policy. Its role extends to provide a robust global networking platform, vital for India's aim to elevate Accountancy within WTO guidelines. Through collaboration and expertise, the Directorate advanced CAs' interests, contributing significantly to India's global trade aspiration.

(I) Initiatives towards Partner in Nation Building Under the aegis of the Directorate of ICAI to pave a long way in promoting accounting and finance services globally; we aim to significantly advance the promotion of accounting and finance services on a global scale. This endeavor will not only open up new avenues for our members to provide a broader spectrum of services in these regions but also enable us to capitalize on various governmental incentives. • ITEC - Professional training program on Sharing the Indian accounting profession story from 18th November to 29th November 2024 under Indian technical and economic cooperation (ITEC) of ministry of external affairs, Government of India at Centre of Excellence of ICAI, Hyderabad- The Institute of Chartered Accountants of India (ICAI), through the Directorate, under the aegis of the Ministry of External Affairs, Government of India, successfully organized its first-ever Indian Technical and Economic Cooperation (ITEC) program titled Sharing the Indian Accounting Profession Story. This 12-day initiative brought together 17 foreign participants from 11 countries, highlighting ICAI's global outreach and its role in advancing the accounting profession over its 75-year legacy. The program aimed to showcase India's journey in accounting, foster international knowledge exchange, and build capacity among professionals globally. • Interactive Meet with Trade Officers of Foreign Embassies on International Trade & Services held on 8th November,2024- An interactive session was held with 26 trade officers from 20 countries and 6 ICAI members representing various industries. Discussions focused on promoting inbound and outbound investment, ICAI's involvement in trade delegations, capacity-building for sustainable growth, participation in the World Forum of Accountants 2025, bilateral trade enhancement, and exploring outsourcing opportunities for Indian professionals in services like payroll, taxation, and virtual CFO roles.

(II) Showcasing ICAI at International Platforms- The Directorate showcased the activities and initiatives of the ICAI in the field of accounting and finance at Accountex, held from 14th to 15th May 2025 in London. The ICAI participated through a dedicated 18 sqm stall located at Hall No. 1255. The event provided an excellent platform for global outreach and engagement. The stall was visited by President of the Institute of Chartered Accountants in England and Wales (ICAEW) and CEO of the Institute of Certified Bookkeepers (ICB). Their presence reflected strong international interest in ICAI's initiatives and further strengthened global professional ties. • Delegation of International Level ➢ During the IFSCA delegation to Sydney and Melbourne, the then Convenor of Directrate met with the Australian Securities and Investments Commission (ASIC) on 5th August 2024. They discussed waiving 3,000 hours of practical training and recognizing Indian CAs under the MRA for supervisory roles under the India-Australia ECTA plan. ASIC assured follow-up updates on these issues. ➢ On 12th September 2024, the then Convenor of the Development of International Trade, Services & WTO Directorate, along with Chairman of the Luxembourg Chapter, met Lead for Digital Trade and Venture, TradeTech at the World Economic Forum (WEF). The discussions centered on ICAI's potential participation in the World Economic Forum 2025 in Davos. ➢ The then Convenor of the Development of International Trade, Services & WTO Directorate, attended the WTO Public Forum held in Geneva from 10th to 13th September, 2024. ➢ Meeting with the members in the Zurich to discuss the legal framework of the approved chapter, operational guidance and other modalities on 14th September,2024. ➢ The ICAI delegation's visit to the United States, from October 2nd to October 9th 2024, marked a significant step in fostering international collaboration and exploring business opportunities in key sectors such as financial services, startups, MSMEs, and accounting outsourcing. The delegation, comprising key industry leaders, visited San Francisco and New York to engage with stakeholders and strengthen the economic and professional ties between the US and India.

9.4 Digital Re-Engineering and Transformation Directorate (DR&TD) Proposal for Making ICAI Head Office Premises Wi-Fi Enabled- ICAI's Digital Re-Engineering and Transformation Directorate has enabled centralized Wi-Fi across its Head Office and Annexe buildings, ensuring seamless connectivity for officials and visitors. The initiative will soon extend to other ICAI offices in Vishwas Nagar and Noida Sector 62, with controlled user access to maintain optimal speed and stability.

Hosting of ISA AT and DMBF Exam Certificates in Digi Locker- As part of its digital transformation, ICAI's Digital Re-Engineering and Transformation Directorate (DRTD) is enabling the hosting of marksheets and certificates for the ISA-AT (Jan 2025) and DMBF (Feb 2025) exams on the DigiLocker platform. Qualified members can securely access their documents online. This initiative enhances accessibility, transparency, and efficiency in ICAI's services Development of Integrated portal for UDIN, CDS and Event Management including CPE, DAAB, and Websites of Board of Studies – Operations under ICAI-TCS DigiGov project- The Digital Re-Engineering and Transformation (DRT) Directorate is currently spearheading a major integration initiative in collaboration with TCS, aimed at unifying multiple portals operated by various ICAI Committees. This includes the UDIN, CDS, and Event Management Portals (covering CPE, DAAB, and BoS–Operations), with a focus on providing a seamless and unified user experience through Single Sign-On (SSO) functionality for both students and members.

Major updates on different portals: • Centralized Distribution Services (CDS)- ICAI's new integrated Centralized Distribution Services (CDS) portal, expected to go live by July 2025, will maintain a Master Inventory of publications, course materials, and souvenirs. It will offer a seamless buying experience for members and students, with real-time tracking of courier and inventory transfers via APIs. A chatbot will assist students by answering CDS-related queries, enhancing user support and operational efficiency. • Unique Documentation Identification Number (UDIN)- With UDIN, the integrated portal is going to provide a seamless friendly user interface to ICAI members in Practice. The members need not have a separate UDER ID and password and can use their SSP credentials to login to the new UDIN system, which will provide enhanced experience in terms features like dynamic Dashboard etc. The Unique Documentation Identification Number (UDIN) portal on DigiGov platform is expected to Go-Live by July 2025. ICAI teams are working towards achieving this target. • Event Management Portal ➢ Continuing Professional Education (CPE)- The new integrated portal will allow seamless registration while applying for CPE portal by about 650+ Programme Organising Units (PoUs) of ICAI and allow them to register for the online and offline Course/Events including any Webinar/Seminar etc. The CPE Portal is expected to Go-Live by August 2025. ➢ Digital Auditing and Assurance Board (DAAB)- Initially, the courses offered by DAAB have been included to the new integrated portal and depending on its success, all the Post Qualification Courses

(PQC), Certificate and Diploma courses offered by different Committees/Departments of ICAI can be migrated to the portal. The DAAB/PQC portal is expected to Go-Live by September 2025. ➢ Student Activities Portal of Board of Studies and Online Registration Portal for ICITSS (IT/OC) and AICITSS (AIT/MCS) courses of Board of Studies- The Board of Studies is developing an integrated, dynamic Students Activity Portal to streamline course management and enhance user experience. The portal will allow different PoUs to initiate courses/batches for ICAI students, with unified login credentials via SSP. Both portals under Board of Studies Operations are expected to go live by September 2025.

This initiative aims to modernize infrastructure, improve document accessibility, streamline operations, and optimize resource use across the organization.

9.6 Financial & Tax Literacy Directorate (FTLD)(under Committee on Financial Markets and Investors' Protection) With the motto "Har Jan, Har Man, Vitiya Gyan se Sampann" the Financial and Tax Literacy Directorate of the ICAI has been steadfast in promoting financial literacy across the country through its inclusive approach of educating individuals from diverse backgrounds, cultures, and demographics. The following key statistics highlight the scale and outreach of the activities undertaken by the Financial and Tax Literacy Directorate during the year:

Program Name No. of Programs Held Reach/Participation Vitiyagyan Mela 2024 150+ 10,000+ Regular FTL Events 240+ 25,000+ Radio Campaign in Vernacular Language 3-Phased

3.95 Cr Programs at Government Institutions Four Senior Dignitaries & Staff Pan India Public Finfluencer Meet One 42 influencers MyGov Competitions (Quizzes, Slogan Writing/Poster Making) Four Engagement E-learning Modules on iGot Platform Two Modules Government Employees Knowledge Dissemination through X Handle/Website Continuous National The Vitiyagyan Mela 2024 The Vitiyagyan Mela 2024 has been a landmark event organized by the ICAI's FTL Directorate through branches and regional councils. Under this initiative, the Directorate facilitated over 150 financial and tax literacy events, reaching more than 10,000 participants. Apart from this, through the wide network of branches and RCs, the Directorate facilitated 240+ regular financial literacy programs throughout the year, reaching over 25,000 people, which included the organization of engaging activities in local dialect and educational sessions. Additionally, through a three-month-long radio campaign focusing on crucial financial concepts executed across the country in vernacular languages, the Directorate reached over 3.95 crore listeners.

Financial and Tax literacy programs The Directorate has even conducted financial and tax literacy programs at various government institutions such as the Governor's houses of Uttarakhand and Meghalaya, Bharat Scouts and Guides, Indian National Science Academy, etc, drawing immense appreciation from the Hon'ble Governors and other dignitaries. The Directorate has brought financial influencers on a platform by conducting Finfluencer Meet. In the event, 42 social media influencers participated, and all of them had exceptionally good followership on different social media platforms.

The event was a testament to the power of collaboration, innovation, and the profound impact of digital platforms on the financial landscape. To contribute to the government of India's mission "Viksit Bharat", the Directorate has always been on its toes by undertaking diverse initiatives, one of a kind is hosting engaging competitions on MyGov, from quizzes to slogan and poster making competitions. e-learning financial literacy course modules/FTL programs To empower the state and central government employees, the directorate has launched two e-learning financial literacy course modules on the iGot platform in association with the Capacity Building Commission. To engage students, young minds, or future generations, the Directorate has even conducted FTL programs at schools and has been a knowledge partner in college events. With the website and X handle offering insight into different financial topics, that too in vernacular languages on the website, the Directorate has ensured that knowledge and information should reach the last mile. Through diverse and inclusive stakeholder engagement, the FTL Directorate is amplifying the impact of its mission, empowering citizens to contribute meaningfully to the country's economic development.

9.7 Grievance Resolution Dirctorate (GRD) The Grievance Resolution Directorate (GRD) works for efficient grievance resolution for our members, students, and other stakeholders with the objective to centralize communication and reduce resolution time and enhance effectiveness through central monitoring. The Directorate utilizes multiple channels for grievance resolution:

Call Sahayata 9997599975- During the period, we responded to over 8.60 lakh calls, majority of which were from students. Apart from Hindi and English, support is offered in 9 regional languages also.We offer help during ongoing critical activities and last dates for membership fee or examinations. This is apart from outbound calls made by us for un-attended calls, including for specific activities like KYM support to members and awareness for the WOFA event 2025. During September 2024 active guidance was given to over 65K and in December 2024, over 9K calls were successfully connected and answered to help members pay fee within extended due date.

E-Sahayata (e-tickets) (https://help.icai.org/)- The students and members can raise tickets by themselves or through call sahayata agents if they are not satisfied with the support or is delayed. Over 50K tickets were raised and resolved through this platform, which is monitored centrally to see that queries are put to the right person and the right resolution is made available within a reasonable time.

Mail Sahaayata (member@icai.in; student@icai.in)- Mails are monitored and assessed to provide a solution through a callback by call sahayata team or raise tickets or to simply re-direct the query to the right person, depending on the requirement.

X @icaigrd- Our stakeholders can even reach us simply through the social media platform "X".

Stakeholder Engagement Following initiatives are being undertaken to make our engagement with students, members, and others more convenient: • SMS Integration: Recently, to strengthen transparency and communication, SMS alerts have been started for grievance tickets through call sahayata. SMS notifications help in confirmation post-call and tracking of tickets easily. • E-Sahayata (e-tickets) upgrade: Working to make the ticketing portal more user friendly. • Focused training for improved grievance handling: To provide better grievance support to stakeholders, students, and members, the GRD conducts departmental training for our Call Sahaayata team based on active monitoring of trending queries. Important updates and frequently asked questions (FAQs) are regularly published and updated on ICAI Call Sahayata – The Institute of Chartered Accountants of India to assist stakeholders in resolving common queries.

9.8 Human Resources Directorate (HRD) The Human Resources Directorate of ICAI has been formed to empower the human resource function of the Institute to be dynamic and act proactively in HR related matters across all offices of ICAI. In that light, the Directorate is expected to plan and execute all HR functions, not limiting to the areas such as Manpower Planning and Recruitment, Transfer & rotation policy, Performance Management, HR Policy & Service Norms Development, Identification of HoDs, Employee Relations & Code of conduct, Training and Development, Compensation and Benefits, HR Information Systems & Analytics, Organizational Development & Succession Planning, Compliance and Legal Matters, Engagement of Consultants, Employee Engagement, Rewards and Recognition, except those matters which are required to be decided by the Executive Committee, in accordance with provisions contained in Regulation 175 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1988, amended from time to time.

Training and Development Training programs for the Institute's employees have been organized across a range of topics, including MS Office proficiency, the application of AI technologies, the operational structure of Regional Councils and Branches, CPE Guidelines and Regulations, Accounts and Audit practices, and the effective use of the GeM portal. Additionally, workshops have been conducted on health and stress management awareness, the Right to Information, personality development, cybersecurity, and the prevention of sexual harassment in the workplace. These initiatives collectively encompass approximately 1,500 man-days of training for regular employees. Further, permission for attaining higher qualification has been granted to eight employees this year.

9.9 Legal Directorate

(I) The Supreme Court in Shaji Paulose vs. ICAI upholds ICAI Guidelines on number of Tax audits under Section 44AB of the Income Tax Act, 1961.

The Supreme Court vide its judgment dated 17.05.2024 upheld the Guidelines dated 08.08.2008 issued by ICAI that had set a ceiling on the number of tax audit assignments which can be undertaken by a Chartered Accountant.

The Court has ruled that the ICAI Guidelines are not violative of the fundamental right to practice profession guaranteed under Article 1(g) of the Constitution of India and the same are in public interest and therefore saved by Article 19(2) of the Constitution of India. Since the 1989 Notification was struck down by the Madras High Court and in view of the conflicting judgments of different High Courts, the ICAI has approached the Supreme Court and filed a Transfer Petition seeking transfer of writ petitions pending in various High Courts to the Hon'ble Supreme Court itself for final determination of the issue. Applying the doctrine of legal uncertainty, the Hon'ble Supreme Court was pleased to quash the disciplinary proceedings initiated against the petitioners as well as similarly situated members.

Liberty is given to the Institute to revise the maximum number of tax audits under Section 44AB. Liberty was also granted to the petitioners to make a representation to the Institute to this effect and the Institute may consider the same. In addition to the aforesaid directions, the Hon'ble Supreme Court in the course of the judgment has paid rich compliments and appreciated the ICAI for playing a significant role in ensuring the dynamism of the CA Course and the role and responsibility of Chartered Accountants in the economic development. The Supreme Court has further observed as under: -

"ICAI has, over time, received recognition as a premier accounting body domestically and globally for maintaining the highest standards…the ICAI has also played a significant role in ensuring the dynamism of the CA course and the credibility of the examination. We commend that the ICAI must be committed towards the convergence of accounting and ethical standards with international standards." The Supreme Court further observed that quality, reliability and objectivity of the information certified by Chartered Accountants are relied upon by stakeholders to make commercial decisions and allocate resources.

Transparent accounting is necessary to strengthen credibility of the financial markets, market participants, investors and other stakeholders. It is, therefore, not surprising to find that the accounting profession is being constantly challenged to meet the ever-increasing demands for quality information or for verification of the financial statements. The higher the quality and integrity maintained by the profession, the stronger and more resilient will our financial markets be. The accounting profession facilities market discipline and renders confidence amongst various stakeholders and reduce the possibility of irregularities in the financial markets. More importantly, Chartered Accountants themselves have to comply with the relevant laws and regulations. The Chartered Accountants must refuse to represent clients who insist on resorting to unfair means. Chartered Accountants are relevant not only to securing corporate governance but governance in broader context too. Chartered Accountants assume different responsibilities towards tax administration, to the client and to the economy at large. In that context, we stress on the importance of preserving their independence of views and integrity to separate their client advisory role from their role as public citizens seeking to improve the functioning of the tax administration of the nation. Integrity, objectivity, professional competence and due care and confidentiality must be the doctrines guiding the work ethic.

(II) Batch of Writ petitions filed in MAF matters challenging the disciplinary proceedings conducted by ICAI Batch of writ petitions were filed by the partners of MAF seeking to be released from disciplinary proceedings being conducted by ICAI as they are not nominated `members concerned' in the proceedings for misconduct as per Rule 8 of the Chartered Accountants (Procedure of Investigation of Professional & Other Misconduct and Conduct of Cases) Rules, 2007.

The Hon'ble Court vide its judgment dated 22nd March 2024 (pronounced on 03rd July, 2024) held as under at para 99 of the judgement: -

"Thus, in a case, where there is any complaint or allegation in respect of a single incident or an act of a member, the firm can designate that particular person, who was associated with the said act, which is alleged to be misconduct. The position would however not be the same, in a case where the allegations are in respect of arrangements entered into by firms with other international counterparts, spanning over decades and multiple agreements. A single individual cannot be pinned down in such situations to be responsible for answering the complaint as `member answerable'. The firm as a whole has to be held responsible if found culpable, in such circumstances, failing which the Act would be rendered toothless." The Court further held that that the writ petitions are not maintainable. It is open for the petitioners to participate in the disciplinary proceedings, if they so choose and file their reply on merits to the notice issued by the Disciplinary Committee of ICAI.

Division Bench of High Court vide order dated 11.08.2024 has granted stay of the disciplinary proceedings initiated against the Appellants.

(III) W.P.(C) No.12096/2019, Federation of Chartered Accountants Association (FCAA) Vs. Union of India & Ors. decided by Delhi High Court Federation of Chartered Accountants Association of India (petitioner) filed the captioned writ petition against Union of India through MCA, NFRA and ICAI, challenging the constitutional validity of Sections 132(2) & 132(4) of the Companies Act 2013, which, inter alia, empower the National Financial Reporting Authority (NFRA) to investigate into matters of professional or other misconduct by Chartered Accountants and impose penalties thereon.

Subsequently, batch of writ petitions were filed by individual chartered accountants who were aggrieved by the punishment orders of NFRA. These petitions were also challenging the constitutional validity of Section 132 as well as the procedure adopted by NFRA in imposing the penalties. All the writ petitions were tagged and heard together by the Division Bench of the High Court of Delhi as they dealt with common question of law. Judgement was pronounced on 07th February, 2025 upholding the constitutional validity of Section 132(2) & (4) of the Companies Act, 2013. However, the punishment imposed on individual Chartered Accountant were set aside on the ground of non-separation of investigative and quasi- judicial functions of National Financial Regulatory Authority

(NFRA).

NFRA has challenged the aforesaid judgment of the Division Bench of the High Court in so far as it relates to quashing of the Show Cause notices and punishment awarded to the individual Chartered Accountants in the Supreme Court. The Hon'ble Supreme Court has refused to stay the judgment of the Division Bench of High Court.

It was also clarified by the Supreme Court that in the pending cases before the NFRA have to be dealt with in lines of the judgment of the High Court.

(IV) The Hon'ble Supreme Court of India on 29th April 2025 in the case of Himanshu Singh Vs. UOI (popularly known as Supertech Case) had issued various directions to Government of India, Ministry of Housing and Urban Affairs, RERA, RBI, CBI and ICAI.

Background:

More than 170 petitions were filed by over 1200 home buyers in the Supreme Court alleging systematic failure of statutory and government authorities to discharge their statutory functions, circumvention of regulatory framework by Banks and Housing Financial Corporations and the resultant illicit benefits said to have been drawn by builders/developers and the cost of the home by us who are now bearing the brunt of such failures.

Directions to ICAI:

In so far as ICAI is concerned, the following directions were issued:

"19. The President, Institute of Chartered Accountants of India is directed to deploy three Chartered Accountants, who are well conversant with the process of forensic audit and place them at the disposal of CBI to assist the Bureau/SIT in the matter of subject enquiry/investigations. The details of the remuneration to the paid to these Chartered Accounts be forwarded to this Court so that an appropriate order in that regard can be passed on the next date of hearing."

The Publication Directorate of Institute primarily caters to following three areas: • Printing of study materials for students and member-related publications. • Sale and distribution of publications through Centralized Distribution System (CDS). • Maintaining Stock account, sales account, and reconciliation of stock.
